---

## Institutional operating model

The repository is the sole canonical institutional authority and durable institutional-memory surface for doctrine, governance, accepted decisions, implementation posture, and material institutional truth.

Cursor is the primary repo operating interface. Codex and other governed agents remain bounded operators, not authority surfaces. HedgrOps remains the governance, strategic-deliberation, and execution-translation layer within its established authority boundaries.

Obsidian may continue as the founder cognition and journaling environment, including governed Narrative Steward review of emerging observations. It is not a successor institutional knowledge base. Material concepts become institutional truth only through the established deliberation, decision, and repo-native governance process.

**Notion is retired.** It is not an active Hedgr governance, institutional-memory, or operating-system surface. Historical Notion-related records remain as history and must not be treated as a live closeout or publishing requirement. See Internal **D-078** / **§207** and `docs/ops/governance/HEDGR_NOTION_RETIREMENT_CLOSEOUT.md`.

---

## 1. Current strategic posture

Hedgr is being built as a **financial stability operating system** centered on the **Hedgr Stability Engine**.

The wallet is not the conceptual center of the system. The wallet is proof of the system thesis.

Current doctrinal framing:

- Hedgr is not a wallet; the wallet is proof.
- The Stability Engine is the system center.
- Governance is the moat.
- Liquidity comes before yield.
- Withdrawal integrity comes before optimization.
- Risk must remain visible.
- Allocation communication must not become shadow accounting.
- Copilot remains advisory and subordinate.

Core operating principle:

**Capital preservation above all.**

---

## 2. Current phase posture

The repo is currently governed by the read-only Stability Engine foundation and governance-hardening posture established across Sprint 2 through Sprint 4.

**Foundation release (repo):** For **Stability Engine™️ - Foundation** as bounded by merged work through `MC-S2-022` and closeout ticket `MC-S2-023`, the canonical assessment in **§6a** records **Ready to mark Shipped** (criteria, evidence table, verdict). That does **not** relax the read-only or non-execution constraints below; it closes governance on the Foundation-scoped body of work only.

**Next release / track (repo canon):** **Stability Engine™️ - Transition Readiness.** Foundation is **closed** as the shipped repo baseline (**§6a**). This track is **not** a continuation of Foundation implementation scope; it exists to define **post-foundation boundary readiness** before any execution, automation, or deeper operational authority expansion is approved. Until future doctrine and approved sequencing explicitly widen the boundary, the **read-only / non-executing** constraints below remain in force.

Current posture:

- read-only
- posture-first
- non-executing
- trust-first
- conservative and reversible
- frontend-centered for current engine work
- governance-constrained

Important active constraints:

- the Stability Engine remains **read-only and informational**
- allocation bands remain **informational**, not accounting truth
- no hidden execution, fund movement, or autonomous reallocation may be introduced
- no ledger duplication or balance truth should be implied by engine UI
- no live backend engine coupling should be introduced in the current phase
- runtime policy and trust constraints remain first-class control layers
- local simulation may exist only as a dev-safe validation aid, not as runtime authority
- **Warmth Layer (ADR 0016, Accepted):** parallel, doctrine-constrained **presentation** refinement only; does not alter read-only Stability Engine posture and must not introduce execution semantics, accounting truth, hidden reallocation, ledger duplication, yield-first emphasis, or gamified stability language (see `docs/decisions/0016-warmth-layer-doctrine-constrained-retail-refinement-track.md`). **Acceptance** is a boundary reference; **§7** / **§7a** still govern any implementation ticket.

**Sprint 3** is framed here as **Transition Readiness** (named track above), not as Foundation continuation: governance defines the explicit standard for any later boundary widening; execution architecture remains out of scope until explicitly approved.

Participant testing is active on the verified ORIENT-002 build; Controlled Parallelism v21 still names only Lanes V, N, and E.

**Completed Lane V comprehension revision (Internal D-086 / §218, amended by Internal D-088 / §220, Internal D-089 / §221, and Internal D-090 / §222; closed by Internal D-091 / §223):** Founder confirmed **REVISE → RETEST** and authorized `CLASS-A-VAL-002-GRAMMAR-001` as a bounded nested Lane V ticket. The implemented candidate leads with the financial-stability outcome, uses familiar and honest financial grammar, preserves Hedgr-owned meaning, keeps infrastructure subordinate without concealing material truth, and removes the synthetic research category contradiction identified by the Human Narrative Lead. Balance uses money grammar, Activity uses evidence grammar, targets use guidance grammar, and Stability Status uses qualitative interpretation grammar. The nested ticket is technically complete and historical. Parent `CLASS-A-VAL-002` remains open; participant distribution remains paused pending live Form verification. Orientation, routes, the Form, engine outputs, target values, ledger / wallet state, arithmetic, frozen narrative sources, Lane E, Lane N, and financial capability remain unchanged.

**Current product doctrine (Internal D-087 / §219; bounded delegation Internal D-088 / §220):** Founder adopts `docs/doctrine/hedgr-familiar-financial-grammar-and-infrastructure-abstraction.md` as active product doctrine: **Borrow the interaction. Own the meaning. Abstract the infrastructure.** D-087 itself changed doctrine membership and precedence only. D-088 now delegates that doctrine into the already-active D-086 ticket within its exact existing Dashboard / Activity file and test scope; it does not create another ticket, modify Orientation or the Form, reopen ORIENT-002, unfreeze narrative, adopt public naming, or widen financial capability.

**Completed Lane V narrative/disclosure coherence amendment (Internal D-090 / §222; closed by Internal D-091 / §223):** Founder amended `CLASS-A-VAL-002-GRAMMAR-001` in place after Human Narrative Lead review identified a category-coherence failure in the synthetic dashboard. The implemented participant path uses one clear visible heading, places Stability Guidance before the optional simulated-deposit prompt, states prototype intent without implying proven capability, removes customer-facing em dashes, identifies targets as planning guidance rather than current money, translates mock / stub infrastructure into human synthetic-research language, and replaces live crypto / investment / deposit implications with accurate synthetic-research disclosure. Default non-synthetic policy copy, required disclosure keys and ordering, material truth, the distribution pause, and every D-086 exclusion remain binding. D-090 created no second ticket, new file, lane, concurrency, public naming, or financial capability.

**MVP phased alignment (interpretation aid):** See **`docs/ops/governance/mvp/HEDGR_MVP_PHASE_ALIGNMENT.md`** for how **`docs/doctrine/hedgr-mvp-project-specification.md`** governance-gated phases and success criteria map to the posture stated in this section and in **§6a–§6c**. That readout is **subordinate** to this file and to **§7** / **§7a**; it does **not** widen execution authority or substitute for **§7** naming.

**MVP acceptance principle (ADR 0024, Accepted):** Institutional MVP acceptance is evidence-gated, not feature-gated. Canonical criteria and evidence recording live in **`docs/ops/governance/mvp/HEDGR_MVP_ACCEPTANCE_FRAMEWORK.md`** and **`docs/ops/governance/mvp/HEDGR_MVP_EVIDENCE_REGISTER.md`**. **Acceptance** of ADR **0024** is a boundary reference only; it does **not** authorize implementation, ticket activation, Evidence Register dispositions, institutional MVP “done,” Class B / Class C execution, or customer-money activity. **§7** / **§7a** still govern any implementation ticket.

**Green Lane delegation pilot (ADR 0025, Accepted historical; operational envelope retired):** The time-bounded HedgrOps translation / disposition pilot concluded under Internal **D-085** / **§217**. No active Green Lane delegation envelope remains in **§6g**. ADR **0025** remains a historical deny-by-default guardrail: Green Lane classification does **not** activate product experiments, create standing parallelism, authorize HedgrOps independent repo mutation, or override **§7** / **§7a**.

Guard both handler and button; accessible amount error and valid correction, including USD 0.29. Do not silently round withdrawal input or change accepted positive Deposit rounding.

### Completed historical nested Lane V build remediation — CLASS-A-VAL-002-REACT-TYPES-001

**Status:** COMPLETED under Internal D-145 / §277 after PR #531 merged, required checks passed and preview/production deployment verification succeeded. The frontend uses `@types/react` 19.1.8, but shared `@hedgr/ui` declares/loads React 18.3.24 and React DOM 18.3.7 types against React 19 runtime peers. A focused TypeScript compiler fixture reproduces four ReactNode incompatibilities at the frontend/shared-UI boundary. A later production build succeeds; the exact remote cache/resolution trigger is not established.

**Exact seven-file scope:**

1. `packages/ui/package.json` — align React/React DOM development types to React 19; runtime versions and peers unchanged.
2. `apps/frontend/package.json` — explicitly align React/React DOM development types to the same versions, avoiding implicit React DOM type-peer selection.
3. `pnpm-lock.yaml` — regenerate with pinned pnpm, limited to the type packages and affected peer-resolution entries.
4. `apps/frontend/__tests__/react-ui-type-compatibility.test.ts` — compile a frontend ReactNode/Suspense/shared-UI contract with the real TypeScript compiler; demonstrate failure before alignment and pass afterward.
5. `AGENTS.md` — bounded authority/current-posture reconciliation only.
6. `docs/ops/HEDGR_STATUS.md` — §7 / §7a, current posture, D-144 activation and technical closeout/evidence only.
7. `docs/ops/bridge/repo-authority-projection.json` — deterministic generation from committed authority; never hand-edit.

Keep the currently retained React type baseline 19.1.8 and compatible React DOM types 19.1.9 explicit in both packages. No runtime React/Next upgrade, type cast, suppressing error, disabling strict checks, skipLibCheck expansion, code/JSX rewrite, CI workflow or provider-setting change. Require distinct reviewer QA, hosted validate/E2E smoke, successful Vercel preview, and production deployment verification after merge. Record the build-context limit accurately. No external messages or participant operations.

### Completed nested Lane V ticket — CLASS-A-VAL-002-CURRENCY-INSIGHT-001

**Status / authority:** COMPLETED — VERIFIED TECHNICAL CLOSEOUT under D-134 / §266. The following brief is retained historical authority only. Original direct Founder approval under Internal **D-133** / **§265**, 2026-09-10: “Proposal approved for implementation. Carry out reviewer QA once implementation is complete.” This accepts the reviewed compressed B design, fixed-rate integration correction, state contract, exact file boundary, narrow observation amendment and normal gated delivery. One active nested Lane V ticket; no Green Lane delegation or Lane E change.

**Route boundary:** Only the existing eligible explicit research Home `/dashboard-synthetic-journey` and `/dashboard?journey=class-a-val-002`. Preserve existing mock-auth/non-live-FX eligibility and unavailable-data exclusion; default Home remains unchanged.

#### 1. Retained design and purpose

Retain compressed B inside the current-position card, above the existing equal-weight utilities. Keep one USD balance, one current display estimate, one local-currency difference and one explanation. Earlier/latest totals belong inside “How this is calculated.”

Retain straight ↑/↓ arrows in the same indigo colour. Use a neutral dash only when a valid comparison produces no displayed difference. Keep higher/lower wording, so an icon is never the sole carrier of meaning. Do not introduce green/red gain-and-loss colours, an arrow beside the USD balance, percentages, charts, forecasting, alerts or conversion actions.

Hypothesis: relating currency movement to the displayed position makes Hedgr’s interpretation role more understandable and personally relevant. Approval establishes the design direction; it does not establish participant comprehension or distinct value beyond a currency calculator.

Approved visual reference: `hedgr-home-currency-b-arrows.html` in the Founder-reviewed exploration. That retained reference remains unchanged. The approved fixture and observation refinements below are explicitly identified for review.

#### 2. Integration correction: preserve the current fixed rates

The exploratory visual used 21 ZMW/USD as its latest rate. The shipped simulation uses 20 and shares that rate across Home, the synthetic Deposit adapter and Withdraw estimates. Do not change that shared rate to match the mockup.

Anchor every latest comparison rate to the existing display fixture. Invent only the earlier comparison rate. Approved higher-estimate case:

| Currency | Latest units/USD, unchanged | Invented earlier units/USD | Latest estimate for USD 300 | Earlier estimate for the same USD 300 | Difference |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| ZMW | 20 | 19 | 6,000.00 | 5,700.00 | 300.00 |
| KES | 130 | 123.5 | 39,000.00 | 37,050.00 | 1,950.00 |
| NGN | 1,500 | 1,425 | 450,000.00 | 427,500.00 | 22,500.00 |
| GHS | 15 | 14.25 | 4,500.00 | 4,275.00 | 225.00 |
| PHP | 56 | 53.2 | 16,800.00 | 15,960.00 | 840.00 |

For lower-estimate test fixtures, earlier rate = latest × 1.05. For unchanged fixtures, earlier = latest. These multipliers define invented samples, not a claim that either currency moved by exactly 5%. Do not display a movement percentage.

Use the same current USD amount at both rates. Round both estimates to local cents using deterministic decimal-safe arithmetic; derive the visible difference from those displayed cents so the expanded comparison reconciles exactly. Classify direction from the displayed difference. If rates differ but the difference rounds to zero, say “No difference at this display precision”; do not claim the rates were unchanged. Reject non-finite or negative amounts and non-positive/non-finite rates.

The shipped candidate uses the single fixed higher-estimate fixture. Other states are injected in tests and separately labelled review examples; no new runtime scenario picker, query parameter, timer or persistent setting is approved. “30-day example” refers to scenario day 0 and scenario day 30, never invented account history or live market dates.

#### 3. Exact compact copy

Normal non-zero comparison:

- Label: **Currency context**
- Period/status: **30-day example · Simulated**
- Higher headline: **[ISO] [difference] higher from the rate change**
- Lower headline: **[ISO] [difference] lower from the rate change**
- Equal-rate headline: **No exchange-rate difference**
- Supporting sentence: **Your USD amount is held constant in this comparison.**
- Visible limit: **FX comparison only—not earnings, purchasing power, guaranteed protection or a conversion quote.**
- Disclosure control: **How this is calculated**

Expanded detail shows both estimates and the exact rates used, explicitly applies the current USD amount to both rates, excludes deposits/withdrawals/yield/asset-price changes, and identifies the invented research fixture, scenario dates, two-decimal rounding and excluded fees/spreads. Retain “No money has moved.” Material limits remain visible when collapsed.

#### 4. Observation coherence

Only when the new currency-context component is rendered on an eligible explicit research Home, and posture is normal with comparison state `change`, replace:

> No other change stands out in the information shown.

with:

> No other change stands out in the simulated activity.

Keep the transaction observation, attention question and non-guarantee caption. This scopes the existing observation to its actual evidence; it does not claim that the engine evaluates FX risk. Keep normal empty/first-event observations, every non-normal posture/notice and all default-route copy unchanged. Use an explicit presentation opt-in with a false default plus the existing research-route check; do not change the global normal-posture string.

#### 5. State contract

| State | Approved behaviour |
| --- | --- |
| Positive ready balance; valid comparison | Render the retained compact summary and matching direction. The USD amount may reflect prior transactions; the comparison holds its current value fixed. |
| First completed event | A hypothetical rate comparison is permitted; never infer that this amount was held for 30 days. Retain the starting-point observation. |
| Zero balance, including after restart | Show “No position to compare yet.” No delta, arrow or misleading flat-rate claim. Keep the existing empty-state observation; add no second deposit action. |
| Hydration, loading or clean-start reset underway | Suppress the currency component until balance and preference are ready; show no stale delta or fallback-currency flash. |
| Pending or unconfirmed balance contributions | Show “Currency comparison unavailable.” / “Waiting for the simulated position to settle.” No directional conclusion. Preserve existing available/pending presentation. |
| Missing/invalid earlier comparison sample | Show “Currency comparison unavailable.” / “The earlier example rate is unavailable.” Keep the existing valid current display estimate. |
| Missing, stale or invalid latest comparison sample | Show “Currency comparison unavailable.” with the specific missing/out-of-date reason. Do not infer direction from the last sample. The independent fixed display estimate remains governed by its existing source, not this comparison fixture. |
| Latest comparison sample differs from the fixed display rate | Treat the comparison as invalid. Never show a second competing current estimate or change the shared display/transaction rates. |
| Invalid balance input | Suppress the calculation and present comparison unavailable; do not convert invalid values to zero. |
| Existing unavailable-data research condition | Preserve the existing route behaviour; do not bypass its eligibility/exclusion with the new helper or display fixture. |
| Valid currency selection | Recompute both comparison estimates and delta together from the same USD total and that currency’s pair; preserve the existing validated preference and fallback behaviour. |
| Non-normal engine posture | Preserve every caution and notice. Currency direction must not change posture, attention level or imply that risk has cleared. |

Stale/missing comparison status is explicit test-fixture metadata. The production synthetic samples do not silently age against the real clock. Missing comparison data does not authorise changing the independent fixed balance-estimate component or the existing unavailable-data route.

#### 6. Approved file boundary

All repository paths in this section are relative to `/Users/musalwa/Projects/hedgr-copilot`.

Runtime, five files only:

1. `apps/frontend/app/(app)/dashboard/page.tsx` — compose the insight inside `balanceHero`; pass the current USD amount, validated currency, existing fixed rate and readiness state; opt in to the narrow observation copy.
2. `apps/frontend/app/(app)/dashboard/CurrencyInsight.tsx` — new research-only presentation component using existing approved icon assets/primitives; no dependency addition.
3. `apps/frontend/app/(app)/dashboard/currency-insight.module.css` — new component-scoped spacing, disclosure and arrow styles; preserve the shared product finish.
4. `apps/frontend/lib/narrative/simulation-currency-insight.ts` — new pure comparison/formatting and fixed synthetic sample helper. Receive the existing latest rate; do not maintain a second latest-rate table.
5. `apps/frontend/app/(app)/dashboard/EnginePostureHeader.tsx` — the narrowly conditioned sentence in §4 only.

Tests, five files only:

- `apps/frontend/__tests__/simulation-currency-insight.test.ts` — new arithmetic, fixture, invalid-data and rounding tests.
- `apps/frontend/__tests__/currency-insight.test.tsx` — new rendered state, disclosure, icon/text and keyboard tests.
- `apps/frontend/__tests__/dashboard.page.test.tsx` — eligible/default/unavailable integration, readiness and retained state/copy.
- `apps/frontend/__tests__/engine-posture-header.test.tsx` — opt-in, route isolation and preserved caution/default behaviour.
- `apps/frontend/tests-e2e/currency-insight.spec.ts` — new hermetic complete-journey, all-currency and responsive checks.

Authority/evidence files for this activated delivery:

- `docs/ops/HEDGR_STATUS.md` (§7, §7a and the corresponding decision record)
- `docs/ops/bridge/repo-authority-projection.json` (generated)
- `docs/ops/governance/mvp/HEDGR_CLASS_A_VAL_002_CURRENCY_INSIGHT_EVIDENCE_NOTE.md` (new)

Read existing `simulation-display-currency.ts`, `BalanceWithLocalEstimate.tsx`, Deposit, Withdraw, Activity, ledger, wallet and engine modules; do not modify them. Existing tests outside the five named files run unchanged. A newly discovered required file change must be surfaced as a concrete scope delta.

Add only `scripts/trust-phrases.allowlist.txt` to the named scope, containing the single substring **FX comparison only—not earnings, purchasing power, guaranteed protection or a conversion quote.** Keep all runtime copy unchanged; no file-wide exemption, scanner logic, banned-phrase, CI, dependency or additional product change is authorised. Commit this authority before RAP generation and rerun full validate, reviewer QA and required hosted checks. This supersedes the file-list exclusion only for that exact allowlist entry. No distribution, financial capability or cross-lane authority follows. `NO CROSS-LANE IMPACT`.

#### 7. Verification and research evidence

Technical acceptance:

- Run all five currencies through the existing equivalent `$0 → +$5 → −$2 → $3` journey. Home’s primary/current estimate and Deposit/Withdraw fixed-rate calculations remain unchanged; only the new comparison reflects the added hypothetical history.
- Cover higher, lower, equal, rounded-zero, zero-position, missing/stale/invalid data, pending state, hydration/reset, invalid/unavailable preference storage and non-normal caution states.
- Assert no currency-comparison writes to ledger, wallet, market, Activity or preference storage; no new network request, subscription, timer or transaction/event emission.
- Check the explicit research route and compatibility route, default Home and the existing unavailable-data route.
- Inspect 320/390/700/1280 px and 200% root text, keyboard disclosure operation and focus, text wrapping and accurate accessible names. Hide decorative arrows from assistive technology because the accompanying wording communicates direction. Verify no icon-only meaning and no unnecessary animated or repeated announcements.
- Run full `pnpm run validate`, frontend production build, protected hermetic E2E and required hosted checks. Retain captures of both collapsed and expanded views and immutable candidate/build identity. Technical results do not establish cognitive-load reduction.

Approved research checks, not changes to the current instrument:

1. Record the settled journey’s unaided baseline before exposing this concept.
2. Ask “What changed here?” and “What stayed the same?” before any targeted explanation.
3. Ask what the arrow describes and whether the comparison says anything about earnings, safety, spendability or available conversion.
4. Ask the participant to explain Hedgr after exposure and relate the information to a recent real currency situation; ask what their existing tools already provide.
5. Present a falling-estimate case after recording the initial response. Treat this as additional exposure, not another independent unaided result.

Record spontaneous understanding, prompted correction, ambiguity and unsupported inference separately. Use behaviour and recent context rather than nationality as a relevance proxy. A small sequential qualitative study can identify usefulness and misunderstandings; it cannot establish causal improvement, market size, willingness to pay or observed repeat use. Avoid teaching the phrase “preserving value” and then scoring its repetition as success.


**Delivery / rollback:** Commit these authority sources before RAP generation. Run the checks and post-implementation reviewer QA above, use normal scoped PR/hosted-check delivery, then record technical closeout and rebind RAP to permanent main in a separate projection-only PR after authority-bearing squash. **Deterministic fixture and preference contract:**

| Display currency | Picker label | Illustrative local units per 1 USD |
| --- | --- | --- |
| ZMW | ZMW — Zambian kwacha | 20 |
| KES | KES — Kenyan shilling | 130 |
| NGN | NGN — Nigerian naira | 1500 |
| GHS | GHS — Ghanaian cedi | 15 |
| PHP | PHP — Philippine peso | 56 |

These are fixed synthetic numerical fixtures, not current rates or FX guarantees. ZMW, KES and NGN preserve the existing illustrative numbers; GHS and PHP are bounded test fixtures only. The mockup example is USD 3.00 → KES 390.00. Compute estimates from the unchanged USD reference and round only the displayed result to two decimals with the ISO code. Never feed the rounded display amount back into financial state. Do not add GHS/PHP to market, provider or transaction-currency configuration. No live-rate dependency, request, subscription or fallback is authorised for this preference.

Default/fallback is ZMW. Store only the validated ISO code in a new browser-local key `hedgr.simulation.display-currency`, separate from `hedgr.market`, wallet and ledger state. Preserve it across route navigation, reload and simulated-journey restart/clean start. Missing or invalid stored values fall back to ZMW; unavailable storage must not block entry and may retain the choice in memory for that page session. No account synchronisation, geolocation, IP inference, currency analytics, server persistence or new personal data. Selection updates only illustrative Position estimates and its preference UI; USD totals, deposit denomination/previews, withdrawal calculations, Activity records and planning percentages are invariant. Emit no conversion or currency-movement transaction/event.

**Required information architecture and copy:**

1. **Why Hedgr exists** — **When the currency you rely on is unpredictable, managing money gets harder.** **What you earn today may not hold the same value tomorrow, and the alternatives available to you can be difficult, expensive or confusing.** **Hedgr exists to make that situation easier to understand.**
2. **What we're building** — **We're building tools to help people understand where they stand financially and make clearer decisions about what to do next.** **We're starting with the Stability Wallet — a simple place to understand your financial position and the things that may affect it.**
3. **Where we are today** — **Hedgr is currently in research and product testing.** **The experience available today uses simulated information. It does not move real money, open financial accounts or provide financial advice.**

The page heading is **About Hedgr**. The three sections above are the complete first-version content budget. Existing product truth may be expressed with necessary grammatical agreement, but no new claim, section, explanation, or call to action may be introduced by inference.

**Placement and hierarchy:** Add one quiet secondary **About Hedgr** row or link inside future/default Settings only. Do not add About Hedgr to `/orientation`, Home, Activity, the persistent primary navigation, the synthetic research Settings surface, or any pre-entry route. #### Completed nested refinement brief — `CLASS-A-VAL-002-GRAMMAR-001`

**Status:** **COMPLETED** (Founder-authorized 2026-08-25; activated under Internal **D-086** / **§218**; amended in place under Internal **D-088** / **§220**, Internal **D-089** / **§221**, and Internal **D-090** / **§222**; technically closed under Internal **D-091** / **§223**). One bounded Class A informational / synthetic presentation revision inside open `CLASS-A-VAL-002`. No second ticket. No fourth lane. No independent concurrency. The implementation is a retest candidate, not comprehension evidence; participant distribution remains paused pending live Form verification.

**Founder disposition:** **REVISE → RETEST.** The current ORIENT-002 experience is not comprehension-validated. Preserve it as the historical baseline; do not reuse its closed authority.

**D-087 delegation, verification, and narrative-coherence amendments:** Internal **D-088** / **§220** makes `docs/doctrine/hedgr-familiar-financial-grammar-and-infrastructure-abstraction.md` an executable interpretation and acceptance constraint for this ticket only. Apply the order **value clarity → familiarity → semantic integrity**, keep infrastructure subordinate and progressively transparent, and preserve material synthetic / trust disclosures. Internal **D-089** / **§221** adds only the existing full-suite smoke contract to verification. Internal **D-090** / **§222** adds the two existing shared disclosure components and policy-disclosure unit test named below so the synthetic research path can remove the observed category contradiction while preserving default non-synthetic policy meaning, required disclosure keys and order, material truth, lane, rollback, exclusions, and retest gate.

**Objective:** Improve clarity and communicate Hedgr's financial-stability role, with value preservation as a major entry-point benefit rather than the complete category, without making the experience intimidating. Use familiar financial-interface conventions only where their learned meaning matches Hedgr's actual semantic boundary, and do not make underlying infrastructure the primary customer-facing identity.

**Highest-value uncertainty:** Can a low-familiarity participant leave the same synthetic journey with the correct unaided model of what Hedgr is, what happened to the simulated balance, what Activity proves, what targets mean, and what Stability Status does not guarantee—without louder, more directive, or more financially suggestive UX?

**Single revision hypothesis:** If the named Dashboard / Activity surfaces lead with a clear financial-stability outcome, use familiar financial patterns for current state and evidence, preserve distinct guidance and interpretation grammar, and keep infrastructure subordinate without hiding material truth, participants will reconstruct the intended product model without corrective prompting or additional financial concepts.

**Evidence basis and limit:** Two governed-cohort responses reproduced the highest-consequence misunderstandings: returns / investment category formation; retention of `$5` after the simulated `$2` withdrawal; 50/20/30 interpreted as funded allocation; and one safety-guarantee inference from calm status language. Treat the baseline as participant-level directional evidence (`2/2`, `1/2`, and contradictions), not percentages, threshold completion, cohort sufficiency, or product proof. The duplicated / unlabeled final-recall field observed in the response readout is excluded only from this two-response synthesis; this record does not establish that the canonical Form drifted.

**Required semantic invariant:**

- **Balance uses money grammar.** It is the sole strong current-state monetary hero.
- **Activity uses evidence grammar.** Each completed synthetic event makes its delta and resulting amount verifiable as visually subordinate evidence, without creating a second balance hero.
- **Targets use guidance grammar.** Percentages remain informational posture, not funded money.
- **Stability Status uses interpretation grammar.** It remains qualitative context, not a score, measurement, or guarantee.

**Named runtime files:**

- `apps/frontend/app/(app)/dashboard/page.tsx`
- `apps/frontend/app/(app)/dashboard/EnginePostureHeader.tsx`
- `apps/frontend/app/(app)/dashboard/EngineAllocationBands.tsx`
- `apps/frontend/app/(app)/activity/page.tsx`
- `apps/frontend/components/TrustDisclosureBanner.tsx` — D-090 synthetic-research presentation only; default non-synthetic behavior unchanged
- `apps/frontend/components/PolicyDisclosure.tsx` — D-090 synthetic-research copy variant only; required keys, order, readiness gating, and default copy unchanged

**Named verification files:**

- `apps/frontend/__tests__/dashboard.page.test.tsx`
- `apps/frontend/__tests__/engine-posture-header.test.tsx`
- `apps/frontend/__tests__/engine-allocation-bands.test.tsx`
- `apps/frontend/__tests__/engine-allocation-bands-panel-copy-contract.test.tsx`
- `apps/frontend/__tests__/activity.page.test.tsx` — required new targeted presentation test
- `apps/frontend/__tests__/policy.disclosure.test.tsx` — existing policy contract; D-090 synthetic-research variant coverage only
- `apps/frontend/tests-e2e/class-a-val-002.spec.ts`
- `apps/frontend/tests-e2e/smoke-pack.spec.ts` — existing full-suite contract; D-089 authorizes assertion reconciliation only

**Governance files:** `docs/ops/HEDGR_STATUS.md` and `AGENTS.md`. `docs/ops/bridge/repo-authority-projection.json` may be generated only after the authority-bearing sources are committed. Nested tickets do not join RAP `active_ticket_ids`; the lane parent remains `CLASS-A-VAL-002`.

**Required revision contract:**

Apply D-087 in this order across the already named surfaces: **what Hedgr helps with → what the participant has → what changed → what it means → what the participant is aiming toward**. This is a hierarchy constraint, not authority for a new page, route, feature, public category, infrastructure capability, or broad copy programme.

1. Preserve `/orientation` as the first informational stimulus and preserve its governed wording. On the synthetic dashboard, the single simulated balance and its directly connected evidence precede qualitative status and target guidance in reading order, consistent with **Total balance → Stability Status → Allocation Split**.
2. A concise Hedgr-purpose cue on the named Dashboard surface must make the financial-stability outcome understandable before system concepts, may present value preservation as a recognisable benefit, and must not invent positioning, adopt a public category name, offer a menu of financial jobs, or foreground crypto, blockchain, stablecoins, investing, yield, or other infrastructure mechanisms.
3. Dashboard evidence must not leave isolated deltas that require unaided cross-screen arithmetic. The current `$3` state must be directly connected to the `$5 → −$2 → $3` transition or to an unmistakable Activity reconciliation path.
4. Activity must derive display-only running state from the governed `$0` fixture and the full, unfiltered set of successful synthetic records in chronological order, attach each result by transaction ID, and only then apply newest-first rendering or deposit / withdrawal filters. Each completed event shows its delta plus resulting simulated balance. Pending or failed records must not change or claim a resulting state, and the calculation must not read from wallet / balance state.
5. Per-event resulting state appears only in the governed synthetic journey. It must remain labelled simulated and must not imply bank-grade ledger authority, settlement, or real-money movement.
6. Stability Status must identify itself as Hedgr's qualitative interpretation of existing engine output. It may not introduce a score, gauge, precision signal, safety guarantee, invented causal factor, or new engine reason model.
7. Target percentages remain exactly the supplied engine values, but their presentation must read as guidance rather than funded cards, pots, accounts, holdings, portfolio slices, progress, or divisions of the simulated balance. No target surface may show currency amounts or `progressbar` semantics.
8. Preserve the calm, cautious, non-pressuring, non-intimidating posture; keep yield subordinate; add no feature, action pressure, explanatory concept stack, or speculative visual grammar.
9. The synthetic dashboard must have one visible primary heading for its purpose, use prototype-intent framing rather than claiming a proven capability, and contain no customer-facing em dash. Stability Guidance must precede the optional first simulated-deposit prompt so interpretation is available before action.
10. Target guidance must say at the collapsed surface that it is a planning position and not current money. The optional synthetic action must remain clearly optional and must not read as advice or a suggestion to move money.
11. The synthetic-research trust-details surface must translate mock, stub, and fixed configuration into human language without exposing implementation badges as participant-facing product identity. Currency display must not imply live market availability.
12. The synthetic-research policy variant must not use digital-asset, afford-to-lose, deposit-insurance, or live-deposit language. It must state accurately that the walkthrough holds and moves no real money and is not a live service. Default non-synthetic policy copy, disclosure keys, ordering, and readiness gating remain unchanged.

**Required preservation:** Existing `/orientation` content and route; `/dashboard-synthetic-journey` and legacy rollback seam; Dashboard → Deposit → Withdraw → Activity journey; `$0 → +$5 → −$2 → $3` fixture and arithmetic; restart semantics; target values; engine outputs; transaction lifecycle semantics; ledger and wallet state; deposit / withdrawal mechanics; mock / stub guards; synthetic / no-real-money disclosures; default non-synthetic policy copy; required policy disclosure keys, order, and readiness gating; frozen narrative sources; Lane E and Lane N boundaries.

**Explicit exclusions:** No `apps/frontend/lib/engine/**`, `apps/frontend/lib/state/**`, or `apps/frontend/lib/tx/**` change; no orientation-source or route change; no Form / questionnaire mutation; no engine field, reason code, threshold, score, state, allocation, accounting, ledger, wallet, or arithmetic change; no target-value change; no default non-synthetic disclosure-policy change; no disclosure key, order, readiness-gate, or policy-engine change; no new feature, asset, provider, market, legal, custody, rail, Class B / Class C, customer-money, advice, or execution capability; no frozen narrative edit, public category adoption, broader product-copy programme, Lane E or Lane N mutation, doctrine change, ADR change, Evidence Register disposition, parent closeout, participant distribution, or comprehension claim.

**Acceptance criteria:**

1. The simulated balance is the only strong current-state monetary hero and is labelled as non-real research state; Activity amounts remain subordinate evidence rather than a second balance surface.
2. The Dashboard makes the current state and its evidence relationship legible without presenting targets or status as money.
3. Activity shows `+$5.00 → $5.00 resulting` and `−$2.00 → $3.00 resulting`, or semantically equivalent synthetic wording, while retaining newest-first display and correct chronological calculation.
4. Pending / failed records do not alter the displayed running result; non-synthetic Activity receives no new accounting-authority treatment.
5. Stability Status remains qualitative, evidence-bounded, and free of score, gauge, guarantee, “safe,” or invented-factor claims.
6. The 50/20/30 values remain unchanged; tests prove no currency, `progressbar`, funded / account / holding / allocated semantics, and rendered desktop / mobile inspection confirms that target structure is visually distinct from money and account containers. This is technical presentation evidence only; participant interpretation remains for retest.
7. Existing orientation, routes, engine outputs, state, arithmetic, transaction mechanics, and financial / execution boundaries remain unchanged.
8. Targeted unit tests, the governed journey E2E, full hermetic validation, named-file scope, and `NO CROSS-LANE IMPACT` pass before closeout.
9. The primary scan of the named surfaces communicates outcome, current state, change, interpretation, and target guidance without requiring infrastructure knowledge; crypto, blockchain, stablecoin, DeFi, rail, protocol, or yield-routing language is neither introduced nor made the primary product identity.
10. Synthetic / no-real-money, interpretation, risk, and capability boundaries remain accessible, timely, and truthful; infrastructure abstraction does not remove or subordinate a disclosure that is material to the immediate participant decision.

**Retest commitment:** Further participant distribution on the ORIENT-002 baseline is paused. Distribution may resume only after a separately recorded verified closeout of this ticket, under the existing governed cohort protocol, and after the live Form is verified to match the canonical approved instrument. The response-readout artifact is excluded only from the existing two-response synthesis; if the duplicated / unlabeled field is present in the live Form, distribution remains stopped pending separately authorized reconciliation. Existing protocol emphasis on unaided comprehension remains controlling. Implementation completion is not comprehension or category-convergence evidence.

**Success / failure boundary:** Success requires independent explanation of a financial-stability purpose, with value preservation as a recognisable benefit rather than a crypto / trading / investment / yield identity; `$3` remaining with Activity as evidence; targets as guidance rather than divided money; and Status as interpretation rather than guarantee. Repeat mechanism-first or investment / returns-first category formation, `$5` retention, funded-target reasoning, prompted-only correctness, or safety-guarantee inference requires another governed disposition rather than a validation claim.

**Stop / rollback:** Stop if clarity requires new positioning, invented engine evidence, accounting semantics, state / arithmetic mutation, Form changes, a route or orientation rewrite, stronger safety or preservation claims, broader yield / investment framing, a new architecture or ADR, Lane E / Lane N intervention, or any live financial boundary. Implementation rollback is one bounded revert of the four named runtime surfaces and directly associated tests while retaining D-086 / **§218**, the distribution pause, ORIENT-002 history, and the two-response baseline for Founder review; then regenerate / rebind the RAP against the retained authority sources. Never restore a pre-D-086 RAP while retaining D-086 authority. Revert the RAP to its pre-D-086 form only together with a D-086 authority-source rollback. Revert D-086 / **§218** itself only if the recorded Founder decision or evidence basis is factually wrong; that restores the pre-D-086 posture unless a new Founder disposition separately retains the pause.

**Governance treatment:** No new ADR expected. This is a reversible presentation and comprehension revision under existing doctrine, ADRs **0013–0015**, and open parent `CLASS-A-VAL-002`. Any durable architectural or semantic-contract change beyond the named presentation scope must stop for separate Founder disposition and repo-native authority.

**Objective:** Determine the minimum information and qualified outputs needed to make one partial-capital reasoning loop understandable and suitable for later, separately authorised product translation.

**In scope — exactly four paired fictional cases, eight states:**

| Pair | Required distinction |
| --- | --- |
| 1. Same envelope, different commitments | Hold the envelope constant; change one stated near-term obligation. Identify which interpretation changes and which facts remain unchanged. |
| 2. Movement across the envelope boundary | Compare an inward and outward transfer with explicit access, ownership and commitment assumptions. Show why balance direction alone cannot establish stability direction. |
| 3. Context changes, balance does not | Hold the envelope constant while an external operating-liquidity timing condition changes. Separate a supported contextual consequence from an unsupported overall conclusion. |
| 4. Relevant information known versus unknown | Remove one material commitment/access fact from an otherwise equivalent case. Preserve supported facts and identify precisely what can no longer be concluded. |

These cases illustrate reasoning requirements; they are not empirical validation, a scored experiment or general model-reliability evidence. For each state record supplied facts, horizon, commitments and assumptions; wider context, intentional envelope, external operating liquidity and in-envelope liquidity; supported interpretation, reason and scope; unknowns, unsupported conclusions and additional evidence required; illustrative Wallet wording explicitly marked research-only/not approved product copy; and dependencies on unresolved policy, D-099 semantics or another governed decision. Finish with one minimum-input/output table and at most one proposed synthetic implementation slice, or an explicit finding that no sufficiently bounded slice can yet be specified.

**Exact files:**
- `docs/ops/stability-engine/research/HEDGR_PARTIAL_CAPITAL_WORKED_CASES.md` — sole new output; retain review findings and revisions here.
- `docs/ops/HEDGR_STATUS.md` — activation, current Lane E naming and bounded delivery/disposition reconciliation only.
- `AGENTS.md` — corresponding operator pointer only.
- `docs/ops/bridge/repo-authority-projection.json` — deterministic generation only.

The §286 decision, functional contract, existing models, instruments and readouts are read-only inputs. No new canonical sequencing file. This exact-file exception adds only the named output to the parent research scope; it does not reopen prior experiments or inherit their evaluator protocols.

**Must not:** Change F1–F4, resolve D-099 by inference, rewrite historical evidence, introduce capacity formulas/sufficiency thresholds/allocation percentages/scalar scores/guarantees, equate deposit direction with improvement or withdrawal direction with deterioration, or treat unknown commitments as zero. No runtime code, executable schema, UI, approved copy, model acceptance, implementation activation, participant data/contact, interviews, Forms, live financial inputs, asset/provider/jurisdiction decision or other-lane expansion. This exercise is not a new gate on already-authorised Lane V delivery.

**Acceptance:** Exactly four paired cases with facts, assumptions and unknowns distinguished. Every conclusion has a traceable basis and declared scope: envelope, supplied wider context or insufficient information. Any arithmetic is transparent fictional bookkeeping, not policy. Preserve supported local conclusions under unrelated uncertainty; withhold unsupported overall conclusions. A distinct Verifier checks consistency, counterexamples, evidence limits and authority boundaries; retain findings in the same artifact without claiming independent behavioural validation. The final synthesis identifies the smallest useful next product question without activating it. Verify unchanged Lane V/G/N, financial permissions and historical research records.

**Delivery / expiry:** Source activation commit precedes RAP generation. Governed review, required local/hosted checks, permanent-main merge and separate verified projection-only rebind precede case authoring. Implementer authors one worked-case pass; a distinct Verifier supplies one bounded review/correction round. Commit authored output and reviewer findings/corrections separately. Deliver through governed checks and return to Founder **retain / revise / remove / retest / escalate** disposition. Unresolved findings remain visible; no automatic extension/successor. Artifact delivery does not close the parent or accept the model. Required delivery authority reconciliation is source-first with separate main rebind after squash; only the four named files may change.

**Stop / rollback:** Stop the affected extension if it requires doctrine changes, new policy, personal data, live inputs, directive advice or another lane's mutation; record the unresolved dependency. Revert only this nested activation and its unaccepted output with RAP reconciliation; preserve parent, historical evidence, current Lane V scope and Lane G deferral. D-143 qualifications, D-099 direction only, §285 audience, frozen narrative, participant pause and release gates remain unchanged. **NO CROSS-LANE EXECUTION IMPACT**.

### Lane E — `SE-REASON-001`

**Active ticket:** `SE-REASON-001` — Stability Engine Reasoning Architecture Research.

**Status:** **ACTIVE** — Founder-approved Class A informational / Yellow research ticket under Controlled Parallelism v21 / Internal **D-076** / **§6f.21** / activation **§204**. Internal **D-084** / **§216** records the Founder `RETAIN / RETEST` disposition for the completed nested evidence review and closes only `SE-REASON-001-EVID-001`. Internal **D-098** / **§230** later activated `SE-REASON-001-RETEST-001`; Internal **D-121** / **§253** accepts the qualifying fixed-instrument evidence and closes only that nested retest. Internal **D-099** / **§231** remains later semantic direction only and is unchanged. Internal **D-143** / **§275** retains the bounded synthetic evidence and closes only `SE-REASON-001-EVID-INTERP-001`; governed delivery checks remain required. Parent `SE-REASON-001` remains open and the models remain non-authoritative; no D-099 semantic revision, translation, product, engine, UX, Copilot, participant-data, live-input, or financial-execution work is activated.

**Current nested posture (§290):** No active nested ticket. §289 is retained and closed; its output exception is consumed. §288, existing models/instruments and historical research bodies remain unchanged. Target semantic responsibilities are reconciled in the existing functional contract by §290; no further research output, model revision, executable interface, coexistence decision execution or product/runtime implementation is authorised.

**Objective:** Determine whether Hedgr can interpret whether a participant’s financial position is becoming more stable, less stable, or materially unchanged from a small, legible set of participant and environmental conditions—without predicting markets or prescribing an asset allocation.

**Research question:** What minimum set of observable conditions is sufficient for that interpretation?

**Type / execution class:** Class A informational research only. Yellow decision lane. Non-executing. No customer-money activity. No widening beyond informational concepts.

**Authority basis:** Founder disposition of 14 August 2026 to activate a bounded parallel Stability Engine research lane + Internal **D-076** / **§6f.21** + this **§7** / **§7a** naming + activation **§204** + governing frame `docs/ops/stability-engine/research/HEDGR_STABILITY_ENGINE_REASONING_RESEARCH_BRIEF.md`. ADRs **0013–0015** continue to bound the Stability Engine as read-only / informational. D-076 does not alter Lane V or Lane N.

**DRI:** Founder — @mhibajene.

**Owners:** Founder / HedgrOps.

**Intended reasoning sequence:** Participant condition → material vulnerability → required stability function → regime interaction → eligible instruments → bounded interpretation → governed action. The research must stop short of live recommendations or execution.

**Required outputs:**

1. Stability Function Model at `docs/ops/stability-engine/research/HEDGR_STABILITY_FUNCTION_MODEL.md`.
2. State Model at `docs/ops/stability-engine/research/HEDGR_STABILITY_STATE_MODEL.md`.
3. Interpretation Tests at `docs/ops/stability-engine/research/HEDGR_STABILITY_INTERPRETATION_TESTS.md`.

Each proposed variable, stability function, or regime distinction must record: what Hedgr believes; why the distinction matters; what evidence supports it; what interpretation would change; what would falsify or weaken it; and a disposition of retain, revise, remove, retest, or escalate.

**Expected disposition:** A founder-reviewable determination of whether the emerging reasoning architecture should be retained, revised, removed, retested, or escalated into formal product / architecture work. This ticket is not expected to produce a complete Stability Engine specification.

**Authorized surfaces:**

- `docs/ops/stability-engine/research/**` — governing brief, the three named parent research artifacts, the two retained D-098-named nested-retest outputs, and only the three D-142 outputs named in the completed nested brief
- `docs/ops/HEDGR_STATUS.md` — activation / closeout reconciliation only
- `AGENTS.md` — parallelism-posture reconciliation only

**Explicit exclusions:** No modification of `CLASS-A-VAL-002`; no nested refinement of the synthetic journey; no new participant-facing Stability Engine functionality; no live macro or market-data integrations; no collection of new participant financial data; no custody or customer-money activity; no automated allocation or rebalancing; no asset or provider admission; no target portfolio weights; no personalised investment instructions; no live recommendations; no production execution; no expansion into property, infrastructure, private credit, gold, or other RWAs; no legal, regulatory, provider, or liquidity claims; no Class B or Class C capability; no doctrine or ADR change by research inference; no Lane N change.

**Acceptance criteria:**

1. The three named artifacts exist as research-only documents and remain non-authoritative.
2. Stability functions are defined with conflicts identified rather than assumed jointly maximisable.
3. The state model prefers the smallest sufficient information set and rejects variables that do not change interpretation.
4. Interpretation tests explain why stability changed in bounded scenarios and do not prescribe a portfolio.
5. Lane V and Lane N remain unchanged; `NO CROSS-LANE IMPACT` is verified.
6. A founder-reviewable retain / revise / remove / retest / escalate determination is prepared. Artifact delivery does not self-approve that determination.

**Stop / rollback:** Pause and return for founder review if the research implies a material change to the Hedgr product thesis; begins affecting `CLASS-A-VAL-002` or another active product surface; requires personal or sensitive participant data; requires live external integrations; needs approval of a specific asset, provider, jurisdiction, or execution path; moves from interpretation toward personalised financial recommendation; becomes material enough to require an ADR; would widen Class A / B / C authority; or begins producing conflicting product narratives. Rollback is revert of D-076 / §204 / §6f.21 / §7 / §7a Lane E naming and removal of unaccepted Lane E outputs; restore the prior V / N concurrency. Do not modify Lane V, Lane N, or financial execution posture.

**Completion boundary:** Delivering research artifacts does not close `SE-REASON-001`, accept the model, change the product, or authorize architecture work. Internal **D-084** / **§216** retains the candidate only as non-canonical research architecture, identifies independent interpretability / inter-rater consistency as the primary remaining uncertainty, and closes only the nested evidence review. Internal **D-098** / **§230** separately activated the bounded independent retest; Internal **D-121** / **§253** now accepts only its qualifying fixed-instrument synthetic evidence and closes only that nested ticket. Internal **D-099** / **§231** remains unchanged semantic direction and is neither tested nor activated. D-142 / §274 separately activated the bounded evidence-interpretation test; D-143 / §275 retains its bounded result and closes only that nested exercise, subject to governed delivery checks. Neither retest closes the parent or authorizes translation. Convergence with Lane V, engine implementation, asset eligibility, portfolio construction, optimisation, and execution remain downstream questions requiring separate disposition and repo-native authority.

#### Completed nested research brief — `SE-REASON-001-EVID-INTERP-001`

**Status:** **FOUNDER RETAIN DISPOSITION RECORDED — NESTED EXERCISE CLOSED; DELIVERY GATES IN §275** under Internal **D-143** / **§275**, 2026-09-14. D-142 / §274 is historical activation authority. The original contract below remains the delivery audit baseline. Only its named readout, authority reconciliation, distinct delivery QA, required checks and permanent-main RAP rebind remain authorized; no new evaluation or successor. The separately authorized isolated retest and excluded initial cohort remain separate. Parent `SE-REASON-001` stays open.

##### Objective and hypothesis

Determine whether independent agent reviewers can use the retained research model to explain bounded synthetic facts and abstain where those facts do not justify a material function change or single overall direction.

**Hypothesis:** With function definitions and decision rules fixed, reviewers can identify the facts supporting an interpretation, distinguish a missing fact from evidence of no change, and expose any additional threshold or semantic rule they would need. Outcome agreement without adequate factual reasoning does not support this hypothesis.

**Type / class:** Class A informational / Yellow; synthetic research only. DRI: Founder. Roles: one instrument author/supervisor, one distinct methods preflight reviewer, and three fresh evaluation agents. The preflight reviewer cannot be a member of the evaluation cohort.

##### Fixed model and bounded methods change

Use the retained pre-D-099 F1–F4 definitions, function symbols, four outcome labels, ordered decision rules, and evidence vocabulary from the `docs/ops/stability-engine/research/HEDGR_STABILITY_REASONING_INDEPENDENT_RETEST_INSTRUMENT.md` (D-098 instrument §2). Preserve the distinction between research direction and absolute safety, sufficiency, or suitability. No scoring, weighting, netting, or new dominance rule.

The explicit methods change is confined to the **new** instrument: the observation under test is described through fictional facts rather than supplied labels such as “material,” “stale,” “credible,” or “conflicting.” This departs from the earlier scenario convention that materiality is declared; it does not amend the original tests or retrospectively strengthen D-098 evidence. Retain explicit controls for observations outside the tested variation so missing target evidence cannot be filled by the general “all else unchanged” assumption.

No operational materiality or freshness threshold is introduced. A dated observation is not automatically stale; a newer statement is not automatically authoritative. Where facts plus retained definitions do not justify the classification, record the missing basis and abstain. Unanswerability is a possible finding about the model or instrument, not permission to create a rule.

##### In-scope case design

Exactly **eight new synthetic cases in four matched pairs**, issued in one fixed opaque order with pair identities withheld. Each pair varies one specified item of evidence while holding the model, horizon, baseline, and other observations constant. Both cases contain factual descriptions; this is not a randomized comparison with the old labelled instrument.

| Pair family | Fully specified case construction | Single evidence variation | What the pair can reveal |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Access within the obligation horizon — F1 | Supply the obligation timing and facts about whether the relevant resource can be accessed by then. | Withhold the decisive redemption/access timing fact while retaining settlement information. | Whether settlement is incorrectly used to fill the missing exit evidence; whether the horizon supports the materiality reasoning. |
| Nominal claim integrity — F2 | Supply the stated nominal claim, relevant event, and direct evidence of what remains realisable. Hold access/runway effects constant using explicit controls. | Replace the decisive post-event evidence with a pre-event observation of the same claim. | Whether reviewers identify the temporal evidence gap without inventing a universal age cutoff or treating transferability as capital integrity. |
| Essential-cost transmission — F3 | Supply the capital/obligation currency relationship and relevant essential-cost facts over T0 and T1. Hold flow effects constant. | Withhold the decisive exposure fact while retaining the same FX movement. | Whether missing transmission evidence becomes unwarranted deterioration or unwarranted stability. |
| Essential cashflow — F4 | Supply the recurring inflow/outflow facts needed to reason about replenishment or consumption of a buffer over the stated horizon. Hold current runway and claim/access observations constant. | Withhold the decisive inflow-continuity fact while leaving an isolated receipt visible. | Whether one receipt is incorrectly treated as a reliable flow and whether the missing continuity basis is disclosed. |

These are approved case specifications, not executed cases or findings. Construct actual case texts only after committing this authority. Any illustrative amounts, dates, or units must be entirely invented, internally consistent, and scenario-local. Prefer direct consequences over borderline magnitudes. No personal profiles, real providers, named assets, jurisdictions, market data, participant data, or product fixtures.

The nominal-claim pair must retain F2's existing nominal-value meaning; it cannot introduce D-099's broader valuation-reference semantics. F1/F4 causal overlap is recorded if encountered, but this ticket does not add a causal experiment, merge functions, or claim that caveat is resolved. Within the four fully specified cases, preregister at least one supported worsening, one supported improvement, and one supported unchanged control, each with inspectable factual reasoning. If those controls cannot be constructed within the fixed semantics and eight-case ceiling, stop preparation and return the design gap for review before exposure.

Coverage remains narrow: missing transmission, evidence predating a relevant event, access timing, and flow continuity. This pass does not establish general conflict-resolution, freshness, materiality, or mixed-function competence. All reviewers see the same fixed order, and seeing both members of a pair may cue the missing-evidence pattern even though pairing is not disclosed. Report those limitations; do not treat eight responses as independent samples, claim a causal improvement over D-098, or infer generalisability from agreement.

##### Exact file scope

Three new research outputs:

1. `docs/ops/stability-engine/research/HEDGR_STABILITY_EVIDENCE_INTERPRETATION_PROTOCOL.md` — preregistered hypothesis, source revisions/digests, case-pair map, factual dependency checks, adjudication reference, methods preflight, and procedural manifest. Withheld from evaluation agents.
2. `docs/ops/stability-engine/research/HEDGR_STABILITY_EVIDENCE_INTERPRETATION_INSTRUMENT.md` — reviewer-facing fixed definitions/rules, bounded instructions, eight opaque cases, response schema, and attestation. No expected answers or historical conclusions.
3. `docs/ops/stability-engine/research/HEDGR_STABILITY_EVIDENCE_INTERPRETATION_READOUT.md` — verbatim locked submissions and hashes as appendices, item-level comparisons, disagreements, evidence limits, and one Founder-reviewable disposition.

Four existing files, only for approved governance reconciliation:

4. `docs/ops/HEDGR_STATUS.md` — current §7 / §7a, corresponding v21 status references, and the new activation/disposition record without widening concurrency.
5. `AGENTS.md` — matching bounded activation/current-posture reconciliation.
6. `docs/ops/stability-engine/research/HEDGR_STABILITY_ENGINE_REASONING_RESEARCH_BRIEF.md` — additive record of this nested ticket and its three outputs; preserve D-098, D-099, D-121, and parent boundaries.
7. `docs/ops/bridge/repo-authority-projection.json` — deterministic generation from committed authority only; never hand-edit.

No other tracked files. The original Function Model, State Model, Interpretation Tests, evidence-review memo, D-098 instrument, and D-098 readout remain unchanged. The reviewed local proposal is not an additional execution output or authority source.

##### Preregistration, reviewer isolation, and lock

1. Commit the approved authority sources before generating RAP or beginning the research outputs. Record the exact retained model/rule sources and hashes in the protocol; copy their reviewer-facing definitions and rules without semantic revision.
2. Before reviewer exposure, lock the hypothesis, eight cases/order, pair map, response schema, reference reasoning, and the comparison rubric. The reference must distinguish factual entailments, permissible alternative readings, missing bases, and prohibited inferences; it is not institutional or financial ground truth.
3. Have the distinct methods reviewer check factual sufficiency, pair isolation, definition fidelity, numerical/date consistency, answer leakage, and opportunities to detect over-abstention. Resolve preflight defects before lock. Do not pre-fill conclusions merely to guarantee author-key agreement. Record preflight findings in the protocol; this is methods QA, not model acceptance.
4. Run exactly three fresh agent evaluations only after the lock. Use isolated ephemeral roots with no inherited conversation, user/project rules, repository or memory context, browsing, or access to other submissions. Supply only the new reviewer-facing instrument. Record runtime/model identity and isolation controls. Ordinary same-thread forks that inherit this proposal or prior conclusions do not qualify.
5. Withhold this proposal, the protocol/reference, pair/source mapping, D-099, prior readouts/dispositions, and all expected answers. Verify isolation from launch configuration and process evidence as well as attestations. If reliable isolation cannot be demonstrated, stop the evaluation stage.
6. Lock and hash all three submissions before the supervisor opens any for comparison. Retain each complete submission in the readout; no consensus editing, negotiated corrections, or answer polishing. Same-runtime-family reviewers provide procedural independence only; no population diversity or human reliability claim.
7. No automatic rerun, replacement cohort, extra case, changed instrument, or model revision. Preserve contamination or procedural failure separately from qualifying evidence and return for Founder disposition.

##### Reviewer response and comparison rubric

For every case, require the response in this order:

1. Relevant stated facts and temporal/transmission links, identified by case fact IDs.
2. Evidence status using retained vocabulary, with a reason; name what cannot be established.
3. Materiality reasoning within the scenario horizon, including any missing basis. Disclose any unstated threshold or assumption instead of using it.
4. Function vector, followed by its causal explanation and one retained overall outcome label.
5. Minimum additional fact that would resolve an indeterminate judgement, or “none required for this bounded judgement.”
6. Weighting/compensation check and unresolved uncertainty. Qualitative confidence, if recorded, remains a self-report and is not aggregated.

Compare at item level: use of actual facts; evidence-status reasoning; justified materiality or abstention; application of retained rules; invented thresholds/facts; missing transmission paths; and whether each pair's changed evidence is handled coherently. A matching vector with unsupported reasoning is a finding, not a successful interpretation. Blanket abstention on cases whose facts support a bounded outcome must be examined as carefully as unwarranted certainty.

Distinguish reviewer error, ambiguity in the instrument, missing model criteria, and defects in the preregistered reference. Preserve reasonable disagreement; do not assume agreement with the author's reference proves correctness. If the reference proves defective, report that limitation and retain it unchanged with the post-lock critique. Do not repair it and re-score the cohort as if the repair had been preregistered.

##### Acceptance shape and decision consequences

**Delivery acceptance:** the three named outputs exist; source fidelity and instrument lock are inspectable; eight cases and all locked responses are traceable; all disagreements and procedural failures are visible; every item is assessed under the fixed rubric; and the readout proposes one bounded disposition. No pass percentage, aggregate stability/confidence score, or statistical generalisability claim.

**Research outcome is separate from delivery:**

| Observed pattern | Permitted proposed consequence |
| --- | --- |
| Facts support coherent interpretations and appropriately bounded abstention across this instrument | `RETAIN` only as non-canonical research usable within these specified synthetic evidence conditions. Preserve residual caveats. |
| Recurring need for an unstated materiality, freshness, or semantic rule | `REVISE / RETEST` proposal identifying the exact missing distinction; no rule is implemented under this ticket. |
| A distinction adds no interpretable value or creates repeated unsupported inference | `REMOVE` proposal for Founder consideration; retained models remain unchanged. |
| Material ambiguity, disagreement, flawed reference, or procedural contamination prevents a conclusion | `ESCALATE` or an explicit inconclusive disposition; do not manufacture agreement or repeat the run by default. |

No outcome accepts model validity, real financial interpretation, safety, suitability, product usefulness, participant comprehension, human inter-rater consistency, production thresholds, data sufficiency, or translation readiness. A successful synthetic result does not close the parent or activate another ticket.

##### Verification, stop conditions, rollback, and closeout

- Documentation verification must check exact scope, links, model/rule fidelity, source and submission hashes, case count, blinding, historical-evidence preservation, and absence of answers in the reviewer packet. No runtime tests are added for these documents.
- At governed delivery, apply the repository's required local validation, distinct reviewer QA, hosted `validate` and `E2E smoke (@hedgr/frontend)` checks, and permanent-main RAP provenance procedure. Passing those checks verifies delivery integrity, not research validity. No deployment behavior change is proposed.
- Stop the affected work if a new model definition, materiality/freshness threshold, participant information, live input, additional case/cohort, personal recommendation, runtime mapping, architecture/ADR change, or cross-lane mutation becomes necessary. Record the gap for Founder disposition. No external messages or participant recruitment are authorized.
- Rollback of activation is a scoped revert of its approved authority additions and generated projection; preserve unrelated worktree drift, earlier Lane E evidence, and Lane V. Retain completed procedural/evidence records unless the Founder explicitly authorizes their removal. A method failure cannot erase an unfavorable result.
- Delivery ends with a Founder-reviewable readout. Subsequent Founder disposition may close this nested ticket only; that approval is separate from activation. Parent `SE-REASON-001`, D-099, and cross-lane/financial boundaries remain unchanged. Reconcile any approved closeout and rebind RAP to permanent main through the normal process.

##### Activation boundary

D-142 authorizes eight cases in four pairs, unchanged semantics, three isolated agent submissions, three research outputs, and a single fixed run. It does not authorize D-099 adoption, human recruitment, a product experiment, or implementation of the research model. Instrument preparation and evaluation remain pending at activation; activation is not research delivery or evidence acceptance.

#### Current Founder disposition — Internal `D-060`

**Status:** Accepted 2026-08-02.
