Founder acceptance Internal **D-063** / **§184**–**§185** explicitly supplied and closed one minimum-effective cognitive-friction reduction pass before protocol-complete dual-layer retesting. Founder acceptance Internal **D-064** / **§186**–**§187** closed the final Stability Guidance semantic-order pass. Internal **D-065** / **§188** adds Institutional Coherence as a complementary evaluation lens; Internal **D-067** / **§191** extends that lens through Progressive Institutional Reasoning; Internal **D-068** / **§192** adds Orientation Before Explanation as the behavioural foundation; Internal **D-069** / **§193** adds Cognitive Stewardship as the enduring learning that interpretation belongs to the institution while judgement remains with the participant. These principles do not authorize product change.

**Decision type:** Product / Narrative / Validation

**Execution class:** Class A — informational and simulated only

**Decision lane:** Green — reversible product learning (classification only; **§7** / **§7a** remain activation authority)

**Evidence basis:** Current `CLASS-A-VAL-002` posture; completed synthetic money-journey work through `HEDGR-UI-005`; adversarial review of `HEDGR-UI-004`; plain-language / low-scaffolding recommendation; current Dashboard → Deposit → Withdraw → Activity experience; remaining product-identity confusion; distinction among interface comprehension, mental-model formation, and category formation.

**Governing product question:** Can a newcomer form an accurate first-principles understanding of why Hedgr exists and what decision it helps them make, while still accurately understanding the simulated money journey and non-live boundary?

**Working category hypotheses (test-only; not approved naming):** “Financial Stability Companion”; functional role — helping users understand where their financial stability stands before they act / financial-position interpretation. These remain test hypotheses, not public or settled market language.

**Intended narrative direction (test framing, not redesign mandate by itself):** Understand financial position → see whether stability is changing → understand why → take an informed next step if appropriate. Dashboard as evidence; Stability status as interpretation; Activity as explanation; allocation targets as guidance; safe action optional and bounded.

**Retain:** Synthetic money journey; visible no-real-money boundary; `$0 → +$5 → −$2 → $3` continuity; simulation-versus-live distinction; protocol-complete retest; Class A / Green boundaries; open parent `CLASS-A-VAL-002`; completed plain-language pass as enabling condition.

**Revise:** Dominant validation question after language pass — from interface-only comprehension toward intended Hedgr mental-model / category formation, with journey comprehension retained as Layer 2 prerequisite.

**Add:** Category-convergence evidence layer (entry/exit identity, problem solved, when/why use, decision affected, delayed recall, unaided explanation without copying Hedgr language). Five-layer synthesis frame: problem recognition; journey comprehension; product identity; decision relevance; behavioural value.

**Remove:** Assumption that accurate explanation of individual interface components is sufficient product understanding; assumption that any unfamiliarity is automatically a UX failure.

**Retest:** Run protocol-complete dual-layer sessions on the verified `CLASS-A-VAL-002-GF-001` build with target participants, unaided recall, category interpretation at entry and exit, mental-model trajectory, transfer, trust-boundary comprehension, behavioural observation, moderator-rescue evidence, delayed recall, Institutional Coherence observation under Internal **D-065** / **§188**, Progressive Institutional Reasoning observation under Internal **D-067** / **§191**, Orientation Before Explanation observation under Internal **D-068** / **§192**, and Cognitive Stewardship observation under Internal **D-069** / **§193**. Observe whether Layer 1 establishes grounded orientation and readiness before explanation, whether Hedgr performs the first reasonable interpretive step, whether understanding then compounds naturally through situation → interpretation → priorities → representation → verification, and whether the participant retains judgement without teaching internal vocabulary, promising safety, weakening agency, or weakening journey, simulation, or trust-boundary evidence.

**Authority boundary:** D-060 does not create a new execution class; authorize customer-money / custody / rails / conversion / settlement / payout / ledger / accounting behaviour; close the parent; establish Class B readiness; approve another UI pass by inference; adopt public category labels; or replace `HEDGR_STATUS.md`, `AGENTS.md`, accepted ADRs, or active doctrine. The completed Founder-selected pre-retest category / narrative-orientation pass was supplied only by Internal **D-061** / **§180**–**§181**.

#### Current Founder product-evaluation principle — Internal `D-065`

**Status:** Accepted 2026-08-03. Adopts Institutional Coherence as an internal Hedgr product-evaluation principle under open `CLASS-A-VAL-002`. Complements — and does not replace — constitutional correctness, cognitive simplicity, trust, product identity, and category convergence. Does not close the parent, authorize product changes, create implementation authority, alter Stability Engine behaviour, widen customer-money authority, authorize Class B / Class C, or create standing redesign authority.

**Decision type:** Product Doctrine / Validation / Narrative Convergence

**Execution class:** Class A — informational and simulated only

**Decision lane:** Green — reversible product learning (classification only; **§7** / **§7a** remain activation authority)

**Definition:** Institutional coherence is the degree to which Hedgr reveals its interpretation, priorities, supporting evidence, and constitutional boundaries in the same sequence that users naturally develop trust. It differs from accuracy (is it true?) and comprehension (can users explain it?) by asking whether the sequence itself helps users arrive at trust.

**Relationship to governed determinism:** Governed determinism explains how the institution reaches decisions internally. Institutional coherence explains how that disciplined reasoning becomes progressively understandable externally. Complementary — one governs institutional behaviour; the other governs institutional expression.

**Preferred review sequence:** Interpretation → Priorities → Representation → Detailed explanation. Prefer this over representation → explanation. Challenge proposals that expose institutional representation before institutional reasoning, even when technically correct.

**Three product tests:**

1. **Constitutional correctness** — Is it truthful? Does it preserve simulation boundaries and authority?
2. **Cognitive simplicity** — Can ordinary users understand it? Does it reduce unnecessary mental effort?
3. **Institutional coherence** — Does this concept appear at the correct moment in trust formation?

**Five-step proposal review:** (1) purpose understanding; (2) institutional reasoning; (3) supporting evidence; (4) constitutional boundaries; (5) retained user agency.

**Stability Guidance illustration:** The completed `CLASS-A-VAL-002-CA-003` / **D-064** pass exemplifies the principle by moving from targets → boundary → meaning toward purpose → philosophy → boundary → targets → detailed distinction without changing the numerical model or constitutional boundary.

**Retain:** Constitutional truth; simulation boundaries; informational targets; situation-before-action narrative; progressive disclosure; category-convergence objective; cognitive-abstraction evaluation.

**Revise (evaluation posture only):** Prefer purpose before mechanics; interpretation before representation; priorities before percentages; progressive evidence; defer institutional vocabulary until necessary.

**Remove (as evaluation assumption):** That additional accurate information automatically builds trust; that exposing more institutional structure clarifies institutional reasoning.

**Retest implication:** Protocol-complete dual-layer participant retesting is immediately next on the verified `CLASS-A-VAL-002-GF-001` build. Additionally observe whether participants naturally understand what Hedgr believes, why, what evidence supports that interpretation, what remains uncertain, and what remains their decision. Strong evidence exists when participants explain Hedgr's reasoning, priorities, and decision boundaries before relying on numerical representation or interface terminology. Capture trust-formation moment / cause and where in the situation → interpretation → priorities → evidence → boundaries → agency sequence reasoning succeeded or broke down — as synthesis observation, not as a scoring system or product-success claim. No protocol change may weaken existing journey, simulation, or trust-boundary evidence.

**Escalation:** Escalate where future work exposes representation before purpose; increases concept density without understanding; strengthens numerical precision while weakening reasoning; weakens constitutional truth for simplicity; or introduces product claims beyond current authority.

**Authority boundary:** D-065 establishes an internal product-governance principle only. It does not alter the Constitutional Charter; modify execution authority; change Stability Engine behaviour; widen customer-money / custody / settlement / conversion / rails / automation authority; close `CLASS-A-VAL-002`; authorize Class B or Class C progression; create standing redesign authority; or authorize any product change by itself. Future implementation decisions may use Institutional Coherence as an evaluation criterion only after explicit repository activation. The term remains internal product discipline, not customer-facing terminology.

**Parent-ticket posture:** `CLASS-A-VAL-002` remains the sole active, singular, and open ticket. D-065 itself creates no implementation authority; Internal **D-066** / **§189**–**§190** supplied and closed only the bounded `CLASS-A-VAL-002-GF-001` pass. Protocol-complete dual-layer participant validation is immediately next. Final product disposition under the parent remains Founder decision following participant evidence.

#### Current Founder product-architecture principle — Internal `D-067`

**Status:** Accepted 2026-08-05. Adopts Progressive Institutional Reasoning as a durable internal product-architecture and evaluation principle under open `CLASS-A-VAL-002`. It records institutional learning from the completed Stability Guidance convergence; it does not create implementation authority, authorize additional UI work, or widen execution scope.

**Decision type:** Product Architecture / Validation / Institutional Learning

**Execution class:** Class A — informational and simulated only

**Decision lane:** Green — institutional product convergence (classification only; **§7** / **§7a** remain activation authority)

**Relationship to Institutional Coherence:** Institutional Coherence was adopted under Internal **D-065** / **§188**. It defines the objective: reveal reasoning in the order people naturally develop trust. Progressive Institutional Reasoning provides the evaluation approach: each layer should progressively increase understanding by answering the next natural human question. This is an extension of the D-065 principle, not constitutional doctrine or a new product concept.

**Institutional Product Learnings:**

1. Graduated fidelity should progressively reveal understanding, not merely more information.
2. Each layer should answer the next natural question created by the previous layer.
3. Purpose should precede representation; numbers remain supporting evidence rather than the first explanation.
4. Institutional reasoning should become visible before institutional machinery.
5. Trust should compound through progressively understandable reasoning, with disclosure functioning as verification rather than primary education.

**Evaluation sequence:** Situation — what is happening? → Interpretation — what does Hedgr think this means? → Priorities — why does Hedgr think that? → Representation — how is that reasoning expressed? → Verification — how can the participant verify the meaning and boundaries? The sequence is an internal evaluation frame; moderators must not teach it as product vocabulary.

**Evaluation dimensions:** Future product, architecture, UX, copy, and participant-evidence reviews should consider constitutional correctness, cognitive simplicity, Institutional Coherence, Progressive Institutional Reasoning, and user agency. The governing review question is whether a layer increases understanding, not merely whether it contains sufficient explanation.

**Validation implication:** Protocol-complete dual-layer participant validation on the verified `CLASS-A-VAL-002-GF-001` build remains immediately next. Observe whether understanding compounds naturally; whether each layer makes the previous layer more meaningful; whether purpose precedes numerical representation; whether institutional reasoning precedes machinery; and whether participants retain decision ownership. Capture this as qualitative synthesis evidence without teaching Hedgr's internal vocabulary, creating a new score, weakening existing journey / simulation / trust-boundary evidence, or treating the principle as proof of comprehension.

**Apply:** Use the principle during product reviews, architecture reviews, UX critiques, copy evaluation, and participant-evidence synthesis. Any resulting implementation still requires a separately activated repo-native ticket.

**Authority boundary:** D-067 is not constitutional doctrine and does not alter the Charter; remove or weaken disclosure; hide material risk; reduce trust boundaries; weaken simulation truth; authorize persuasive behavioural design; modify target values or semantics; change Stability Engine behaviour; widen financial state, customer-money, custody, provider, rail, conversion, settlement, payout, execution, advice, recommendation, or automation authority; authorize UI work or redesign; close `CLASS-A-VAL-002`; activate a successor or nested ticket; or authorize Class B / Class C progression. Green classification does not substitute for repo-native activation.

**Parent-ticket posture:** `CLASS-A-VAL-002` remains the sole active, singular, and open ticket. `CLASS-A-VAL-002-GF-001` remains completed historical authority under Internal **D-066** / **§189**–**§190**. No nested refinement is active. Proceed to protocol-complete dual-layer participant validation on the verified GF-001 build, followed by governed evidence synthesis and Founder disposition. No further refinement may be inferred.

#### Current Founder product-architecture principle — Internal `D-068`

**Status:** Accepted 2026-08-05. Adopts Orientation Before Explanation as a durable internal product-architecture and evaluation principle under open `CLASS-A-VAL-002`. It records institutional learning from the completed Stability Guidance convergence; it does not create implementation authority, a new product requirement, or additional UI work.

**Decision type:** Product Architecture / Institutional Learning / Experience Philosophy

**Execution class:** Class A — informational and simulated only

**Decision lane:** Green — institutional product convergence (classification only; **§7** / **§7a** remain activation authority)

**Definition:** The interface should first help a participant understand where they are, whether the observable interpretation indicates that anything requires attention, and that deeper understanding can unfold progressively. Layer 1 establishes enough grounded orientation to continue; it does not attempt to explain Hedgr or complete institutional reasoning.

**Institutional Product Learnings:**

1. Orientation precedes explanation; the first interaction should reduce uncertainty before increasing knowledge.
2. Trust begins before full understanding, and progressively understandable reasoning can deepen that trust.
3. Layer 1 establishes a relationship of grounded orientation rather than complete comprehension: “I understand enough to continue,” not “I now understand Hedgr.”
4. Evidence-grounded institutional reassurance should precede deeper institutional reasoning.
5. Explanation should feel invited by the participant's next natural question rather than imposed through institutional terminology.

**Layer 1 evaluation:** Ask whether the participant feels oriented, calmer, supported, capable of understanding progressively, and ready to continue without being required to decode institutional machinery. These are qualitative evaluation dimensions, not promises, scores, or product claims.

**Evidence-grounded reassurance correction:** Language such as “safe to continue” or “nothing urgent requires your attention” is valid only when supported by the current observable interpretation. Where the state is uncertain or indicates required attention, Layer 1 must orient the participant to that uncertainty or attention requirement. D-068 does not authorize reassurance without evidence, false confidence, or concealment of material risk.

**Relationship to existing principles:** Constitutional correctness preserves truth → Institutional Coherence asks whether reasoning unfolds naturally → Graduated Fidelity deepens understanding progressively → Progressive Institutional Reasoning answers each next natural question → Orientation Before Explanation supplies the behavioural foundation by establishing grounded readiness before deeper reasoning. D-068 does not amend doctrine or replace any prior principle.

**Financial Stability Companion boundary:** D-068 uses “Financial Stability Companion” only as an internal behavioural frame and the existing test-only category hypothesis: calm orientation, progressive understanding, institutional reasoning, and preserved agency. It does not adopt a settled or public category label, authorize customer-facing naming, or prove category convergence. The phrase must remain unshown during unaided participant-category elicitation.

**Validation implication:** Protocol-complete dual-layer participant validation on the verified `CLASS-A-VAL-002-GF-001` build remains immediately next. Observe whether the initial surface reduces uncertainty, makes the participant ready to continue, accurately signals attention or uncertainty, and invites rather than imposes deeper explanation. Capture this as qualitative synthesis evidence without teaching Hedgr's internal vocabulary, creating a new score, weakening existing journey / simulation / trust-boundary evidence, or treating reassurance as proof of trust.

**Apply:** Use the principle during future product-architecture, UX, narrative, validation-synthesis, and product-convergence reviews. Any resulting implementation still requires a separately activated repo-native ticket.

**Authority boundary:** D-068 is not constitutional doctrine and does not alter the Charter; authorize implementation or product requirements; create a new product concept; adopt public naming; authorize artificial friendliness, conversational personality, emotional manipulation, unsupported reassurance, or persuasive behavioural design; soften constitutional truth; hide material uncertainty or risk; modify target values or semantics; change Stability Engine behaviour; widen financial state, customer-money, custody, provider, rail, conversion, settlement, payout, execution, advice, recommendation, or automation authority; authorize UI work or redesign; close `CLASS-A-VAL-002`; activate a successor or nested ticket; or authorize Class B / Class C progression. Green classification does not substitute for repo-native activation.

**Parent-ticket posture:** `CLASS-A-VAL-002` remains the sole active, singular, and open ticket. `CLASS-A-VAL-002-GF-001` remains completed historical authority under Internal **D-066** / **§189**–**§190**. No nested refinement is active. Proceed to protocol-complete dual-layer participant validation on the verified GF-001 build, followed by governed evidence synthesis and Founder disposition. No further refinement may be inferred.

#### Current Founder institutional product learning — Internal `D-069`

**Status:** Accepted 2026-08-05. Adopts Cognitive Stewardship as an enduring internal institutional product learning under open `CLASS-A-VAL-002`. It preserves behavioural architecture learned through Constitutional review, Institutional Coherence, Graduated Fidelity, Progressive Institutional Reasoning, Orientation Before Explanation, and the Layer 1 Institutional Warmth review. It does not create doctrine, implementation requirements, execution authority, product specifications, or additional UI work.

**Decision type:** Institutional Product Learning / Product Architecture

**Execution class:** Class A — informational and simulated only

**Decision lane:** Green — institutional product convergence (classification only; **§7** / **§7a** remain activation authority)

**Definition:** The institution should progressively carry the work of interpretation while progressively returning the work of judgement to the participant. Hedgr should reduce unnecessary cognitive effort without removing participant agency.

**Accepted institutional learnings:**

1. Graduated Fidelity should progressively reveal more understanding, not merely more information.
2. Each layer should answer the next natural question created by the previous layer.
3. Purpose should precede representation.
4. Institutional reasoning should precede institutional machinery.
5. Trust compounds through progressively understandable reasoning rather than disclosure volume alone.
6. The institution should carry the first cognitive step; the participant should retain the decision.
7. The institution should reduce uncertainty before increasing understanding.

**Behavioural model:** Intended progression is Institution observes → Institution interprets → Institution explains → Participant understands → Participant decides. Challenge the alternative where the institution presents and leaves the participant to perform interpretation before understanding. This model assigns interpretation to Hedgr and judgement to the participant; neither responsibility should migrate to the other.

**Coherent architecture stack:** Constitutional correctness preserves truth → Institutional Coherence reveals reasoning in trust-forming order → Graduated Fidelity reveals understanding progressively → Progressive Institutional Reasoning answers the next natural human question → Orientation Before Explanation reduces uncertainty before deeper reasoning → Cognitive Stewardship makes the institution carry interpretation while the participant retains judgement. D-069 records one coherent internal philosophy; it does not amend doctrine.

**Evaluation framework:** Future product-architecture, UX, interaction, copy, validation-synthesis, and convergence reviews should ask who is carrying the cognitive work; whether the institution reduced uncertainty before increasing understanding; whether participant judgement remains intact; and whether the participant feels accompanied through understanding rather than merely presented with information.

**Financial Stability Companion boundary:** Cognitive Stewardship defines companionship through disciplined institutional behaviour — carrying interpretation, progressively revealing understanding, reducing uncertainty, and preserving participant judgement — not conversational AI, personality, coaching, emotional design, or persuasion. “Financial Stability Companion” remains an internal behavioural frame and test-only category hypothesis, not settled or public naming, and must remain unshown during unaided participant-category elicitation.

**Validation implication:** Protocol-complete dual-layer participant validation on the verified `CLASS-A-VAL-002-GF-001` build remains immediately next. Qualitatively observe whether Hedgr performs the first reasonable interpretive step, reduces unnecessary cognitive effort across layers, makes its interpretation distinguishable from the participant's judgement, and preserves decision ownership. Do not teach internal terminology, create a score, treat the learning as proof of comprehension or trust, weaken disclosure or material uncertainty, or infer implementation authority.

**Apply:** Use Cognitive Stewardship as an enduring evaluation principle that guides institutional judgement rather than prescribing implementation. Any resulting implementation still requires a separately activated repo-native ticket.

**Authority boundary:** D-069 is non-doctrinal, non-authoritative, and implementation-independent. It does not alter the Constitutional Charter; create product specifications or requirements; remove or weaken disclosure; hide material uncertainty; imply certainty beyond observable interpretation; increase persuasion; create artificial warmth or emotional manipulation; reduce participant agency; modify target values or semantics; change Stability Engine behaviour; widen financial state, customer-money, custody, provider, rail, conversion, settlement, payout, execution, advice, recommendation, or automation authority; authorize UI, copy, product, architecture, or backend work; close `CLASS-A-VAL-002`; activate a successor or nested ticket; adopt public naming; or authorize Class B / Class C progression. Green classification does not substitute for repo-native activation.

**Parent-ticket posture:** `CLASS-A-VAL-002` remains the sole active, singular, and open ticket. `CLASS-A-VAL-002-GF-001` remains completed historical authority under Internal **D-066** / **§189**–**§190**. No nested refinement is active. Proceed to protocol-complete dual-layer participant validation on the verified GF-001 build, followed by governed evidence synthesis and Founder disposition. No further refinement may be inferred.

#### Completed nested refinement — `HEDGR-UI-005`

**Status:** Implemented and verified 2026-08-01 as the final pre-retest plain-language and information-order Class A presentation / copy pass within `CLASS-A-VAL-002`; activation **§177**; closeout **§178**; Internal **D-059**. The parent remains the sole active ticket and remains open.

**Relationship to the parent ticket:** `HEDGR-UI-005` is the completed Founder-authorized final nested language-friction pass under Internal **D-059**. It is not a concurrent lane, successor, independently active ticket, parent closeout, or comprehension claim. Sequence was later amended by Internal **D-060** / **D-061** to insert nested `HEDGR-UI-006` before dual-layer retest. Nested closeout alone does not close the parent.

**Objective:** Reduce avoidable institutional-language friction and premature explanation of the complete journey so the protocol-complete retest can distinguish category unfamiliarity from money-journey misunderstanding, while preserving the simulated `$0 → +$5 → −$2 → $3` arithmetic, visible simulated-state boundaries, and non-live capability posture.

**Primary product question for this nested pass:** Can ordinary money language reduce avoidable decoding friction while preserving an independent test of whether participants form the correct simulated-money mental model?

**Type / execution class:** Nested product-validation refinement; Class A informational / synthetic presentation and copy only. Non-executing for customer money.

**Authority basis:** Founder acceptance of Internal **D-059** + activation **§177** + this **§7a** nested brief + parent `CLASS-A-VAL-002` authority + Internal **D-057** / **§174** (parent remains open) + completed nested `HEDGR-UI-004` / **D-058** / **§175**–**§176** (historical only) + ADRs **0013–0015** + ADR **0024** (evidence principle only) + existing synthetic journey surfaces + participant protocol `docs/ops/governance/mvp/HEDGR_CLASS_A_VAL_002_PARTICIPANT_PROTOCOL.md`.

**Plain-language standard (required information order per action):**

1. What is happening
2. What visible result follows
3. What is not happening

Explain the immediate step; do not narrate the complete four-step relationship before the participant experiences it. Reveal future-step relationships after confirmation or when they become relevant.

**Approved terminology direction (primary consumer terms):**

| Current term | Preferred primary consumer term |
| --- | --- |
| local fixture record | simulated deposit / simulated withdrawal |
| local fixture balance | simulated balance |
| fixture arithmetic | example calculation |
| synthetic preview rate | example rate |
| target shares | stability targets |
| informational posture | guidance only |
| external settlement | real payout |
| settlement service | bank or payment provider |
| synthetic deposit record | simulated deposit |
| synthetic withdrawal record | simulated withdrawal |

“Synthetic” may remain in research, protocol, governance, and internal labels. “Simulated” is the consistent customer-facing term. Do not reduce “simulated balance” to “balance.” Do not state forms such as “Your money becomes $5.” Prefer: “The simulation shows ZMW 100 as $5.00.”

**In scope:**

- Consumer-facing terminology; sentence length; information order; primary and secondary copy hierarchy.
- Placement and repetition of trust-boundary statements.
- Labels for simulated balances, example rates, deposits, withdrawals, and visible arithmetic.
- Timing of explanatory copy as the participant moves through the journey.
- Removal of unnecessary repetition where the synthetic / simulated boundary remains visible at the point of financial meaning.
- Protocol capture fields necessary to distinguish unaided understanding from scaffolding dependence, where those edits remain inside the parent validation scope.
- Ticket-relevant unit / Playwright updates and local design-qa evidence for the presentation / copy change.

**Authorized implementation surfaces:**

- `apps/frontend/app/(app)/dashboard/**`
- `apps/frontend/app/(app)/deposit/page.tsx`
- `apps/frontend/app/(app)/withdraw/page.tsx`
- `apps/frontend/app/(app)/activity/page.tsx`
- `apps/frontend/app/(app)/layout.tsx` and `apps/frontend/app/(app)/layout.client.tsx` — shared trust / navigation shell copy only
- Existing ticket-relevant modules under `apps/frontend/components/`, `apps/frontend/lib/state/`, and `apps/frontend/lib/tx/` — consumer-facing labels and trust continuity copy only when required by the plain-language pass
- Ticket-relevant frontend unit and Playwright tests under `apps/frontend/__tests__/` and `apps/frontend/tests-e2e/`
- `docs/ops/governance/mvp/HEDGR_CLASS_A_VAL_002_PARTICIPANT_PROTOCOL.md` — retest capture-field refinement only
- `design-qa.md` — local, non-authoritative visual-comparison evidence only
- `docs/ops/HEDGR_STATUS.md` and `docs/ops/NOTION_GOVERNANCE_STAGING.md` — activation and later closeout reconciliation only

`apps/frontend/lib/engine/**` remains outside the default implementation surface. Any required engine-facing presentation change must stop for explicit scope confirmation and follow the engine-governance read order before editing.

**Must not:**

- Change financial state, arithmetic, state transitions, navigation, journey order, Dashboard IA beyond copy hierarchy, Stability Engine posture, allocation or target semantics, Activity record structure, unavailable-data behaviour, withdrawal status logic, or live-versus-simulated capability boundaries.
- Modify backend or engine modules; ledger or accounting semantics; provider, custody, rail, payout, conversion, or settlement behaviour; broader visual design; product category strategy; or participant success thresholds.
- Close `CLASS-A-VAL-002`, claim participant comprehension, accept Evidence Register evidence, clear a gate, activate Class B / Class C, complete institutional MVP, nominate a successor, or authorize another UI pass after this one.
- Imply real conversion, payout, allocation, account balance, or fund movement.
- Open a concurrent lane or treat this nested pass as independent sequencing authority.

**Acceptance and verification:**

- No primary instruction requires knowledge of Hedgr’s implementation model.
- “Fixture,” “informational posture,” “settlement,” and equivalent institutional terms are removed from primary consumer UI.
- Each step can be completed without the full journey model being stated in advance.
- Financial state remains visibly simulated; “simulated” remains present wherever balances, deposits, withdrawals, rates, Activity entries, or money changes are shown.
- Disclaimers are concise, contextual, and non-repetitive; future-step relationships appear after or when relevant.
- No copy implies real conversion, payout, allocation, account balance, or fund movement.

- Existing unit, integration, end-to-end, lint, and build checks relevant to the edited surfaces pass.
- The PR explicitly states that implementation completion does not demonstrate comprehension.

**Stop / rollback:** Stop and escalate if work changes navigation, state, arithmetic, IA, engine meaning, Activity structure, broader visual design, or execution semantics; if trust boundaries become less explicit; if copy implies real financial activity; or if scope cannot remain isolated from financial logic. - Refine the Primary Stability Edge on Stable balance only as a restrained, token-based hierarchy cue that remains legible without colour and cannot read as a progress rail, chart axis, allocation bar, safety badge, alert, or runtime-state indicator.
- Use only approved `DESIGN.md` tokens or canonical `semanticStatus` aliases for restrained tonal or semantic differentiation; labels, copy, structure, spacing, and component hierarchy remain the primary carriers of meaning.
- Strengthen accessible progressive disclosure for secondary simulation explanation, longer target detail, review detail, risk context, and institutional disclosures while keeping the persistent synthetic / no-real-money statement continuously visible.
- Promote Review snapshot after Stability structure and distinguish current simulation status, fixture target date, and last viewed locally without changing fixture dates, fingerprints, memory behavior, posture, or state logic.
- Consolidate repeated informational-target, ledger-balance, non-executed-movement, protection, and withdrawal framing without removing or materially rewriting substantive trust claims.
- Refine spacing, content width, typography hierarchy, whitespace, card separation, and desktop / mobile rhythm for scanning.
- Update the participant-review materials only to test first-read comprehension, primary-action recognition, lane interpretation, informational-target versus balance understanding, no-real-money recognition, temporal clarity, trust, usefulness, colour interpretation, and disclosure confidence.
- Confirm terminology, balance, state, navigation, and trust coherence across Dashboard, Deposit, Withdraw, Activity, and the shared simulation boundary.

**Authorized implementation surfaces:**

- `apps/frontend/app/(app)/dashboard/**`
- Minimum existing shared simulation or navigation presentation required to preserve the authorized journey's consistency; no new route or journey step
- Ticket-relevant tests under `apps/frontend/__tests__/` and `apps/frontend/tests-e2e/`
- Existing `CLASS-A-VAL-002` participant-review materials under `docs/ops/`
- `design-qa.md` and ticket-specific local visual evidence
- `docs/ops/HEDGR_STATUS.md` — this activation and later closeout reconciliation only

**Semantic freeze / must not:**

- Do not change `NORMAL`, introduce runtime states, redefine Stable balance / Conservative yield / Reserve, change allocation-target interpretation, rewrite trust or protection meaning, change fixtures or dates, alter the journey sequence, enable or disable controls, change routing, or change withdrawal / settlement / execution meaning.
- Do not modify `apps/frontend/lib/engine/**`, backend, ledger mutation, providers, banking, custody, rails, production FX, settlement, payout, customer-money paths, Settings, or Copilot.
- Do not add tokens, palette values, raw status hues, gradients, alert systems, performance framing, safety guarantees, urgency, execution cues, or gamification.
- Do not implement the Stability Condition Interface, new `EnginePosture` values, allocation / rebalancing logic, Evidence Register acceptance, Class B feasibility approval, institutional MVP completion, production readiness, or participant-comprehension claims.

**Acceptance and verification:** The journey remains traversable and visibly synthetic; Stable balance is the immediate visual anchor; supporting lanes remain subordinate and understandable without colour; the Primary Stability Edge remains hierarchy-only; one primary journey action remains dominant; Review snapshot is visible without competing with primary content; current simulation state, fixture target date, and local viewing history are distinguishable; disclosure meaning remains intact; desktop and mobile remain coherent; terminology remains consistent across the full journey; relevant unit, Playwright, lint, typecheck, accessibility, contrast, and visual-comparison evidence is reported truthfully; interface completion alone does not close the parent.

**Stop / rollback:** Stop on any required engine-library or runtime-state edit, new token or semantic alias, Paper / repo semantic conflict, status-signalling Primary Stability Edge, substantive disclosure reinterpretation, new product meaning, live-financial assumption, or participant-evidence / Class B claim. Rollback is one bounded revert of the presentation, tests, participant-material refinement, visual evidence, and this nested-refinement record while leaving `CLASS-A-VAL-002` active and its existing implementation evidence intact.

- `docs/ops/HEDGR_STATUS.md` — this nested-refinement activation and later closeout reconciliation only

**Must not:**

**Stop / rollback:** Stop on any required engine-library or runtime-logic edit, brand-token conflict, new disclosure meaning, broader Condition Interface adoption, or participant-evidence / Class B claim. Rollback is a single revert of the Dashboard presentation, tests, local QA evidence, and this nested-refinement record while leaving `CLASS-A-VAL-002` active and its prior implementation evidence intact.

**Required participant outcomes:**

1. Explain what Hedgr is intended to do.
2. Distinguish informational targets from ledger balances.
3. Understand the purpose of the Stability Engine posture.
4. Follow the synthetic journey from balance through withdrawal and activity.
5. Correctly state that no real money moved.
6. Identify what action is available and what remains unavailable.
7. Express whether the proposition is useful and trustworthy enough to continue exploring.

**In scope:**

- Establish internally consistent governed synthetic balance and transaction states.
- Provide uninterrupted traversal through Dashboard, Deposit, Withdraw, and Activity.
- Align the product narrative across the authorized route slice and shared trust/navigation shell.
- Preserve and clarify the persistent simulation boundary.
- Remove contradictions that prevent valid participant interpretation.
- Create one bounded participant-validation protocol and evidence-capture method under `docs/ops/governance/mvp/`.
- Include a secondary blocked or unavailable-data scenario for trust and exception testing.
- Make only the minimum presentation and shared-component changes required to support the validation journey.

**Primary validation condition:** The primary participant journey must be a visibly synthetic end-to-end state. The existing blocked FX or deposit state may remain only as a secondary exception scenario; it must not be the sole or primary product-proposition condition.

**Explicitly excluded:**

- Live money movement; custody; provider or banking integrations; production FX; settlement or payout capability.
- Ledger mutation beyond governed synthetic fixtures; Stability Engine execution; Class B implementation.
- Broad brand redesign; general dashboard beautification; unrelated accessibility or design-system remediation.
- Expansion of Copilot functionality or customer productisation of Settings.
- Settings and Copilot in the primary participant journey unless separately authorized.
- Launch-market, provider, rail, custody, chain, or stablecoin selection; Kenya counsel send; Evidence Register acceptance; gate clearance; institutional MVP completion.

**Authorized implementation surfaces (must be narrowed to the minimum actual patch):**

- `apps/frontend/app/(app)/dashboard/**`
- `apps/frontend/app/(app)/deposit/page.tsx`
- `apps/frontend/app/(app)/withdraw/page.tsx`
- `apps/frontend/app/(app)/activity/page.tsx`
- `apps/frontend/app/(app)/layout.tsx` and `apps/frontend/app/(app)/layout.client.tsx` — shared trust/navigation shell only
- Existing ticket-relevant modules under `apps/frontend/components/`, `apps/frontend/lib/state/`, and `apps/frontend/lib/tx/` — governed synthetic state and shared trust presentation only
- `packages/ui/src/EmptyState.tsx` and package export/test surfaces only if required by the primary journey
- Ticket-relevant frontend unit and Playwright tests under `apps/frontend/__tests__/` and `apps/frontend/tests-e2e/`
- One participant protocol / evidence-capture artifact under `docs/ops/governance/mvp/`
- `docs/ops/HEDGR_STATUS.md` and `docs/ops/NOTION_GOVERNANCE_STAGING.md` for closeout reconciliation only

`apps/frontend/lib/engine/**` is outside the default implementation surface. Any required engine-facing presentation change must stop for explicit scope confirmation and follow the engine-governance read order before editing.

**Acceptance criteria:**

- The authorized synthetic journey is traversable end to end with no unavoidable blocked loop in the primary condition.
- Balances, transaction states, and Activity records are internally consistent.
- The simulation boundary remains persistent and unambiguous.
- Informational allocations cannot reasonably be interpreted as completed movement.
- Withdrawal states do not imply live settlement or payout.
- Settings and Copilot are removed from or clearly excluded from the primary validation narrative.
- Desktop and mobile preserve the core journey and required context.
- The participant protocol and evidence-capture method are documented.
- Tests cover governed synthetic states and prevent accidental live-capability implication.
- Implementation remains within Class A authority.

**Evidence required at closeout:**

- Screenshots or recordings of the complete synthetic journey.
- Test evidence for the authorized states.
- Confirmation that no execution authority or live integration was introduced.
- Participant-session script, comprehension and interpretation questions, and evidence-capture template.
- Explicit mapping from evidence collected to the future Class B feasibility decision.

**Decision unlocked:** Evidence may support Founder disposition to (1) proceed to a one-market Class B feasibility proposal, (2) use the authorized Class A presentation iteration to address one evidenced comprehension failure, (3) reframe the proposition or target cohort, or (4) pause the lane. Interface completion alone does not close this ticket.

**Stop / rollback:** Stop if work requires live financial capability, provider / banking integration, production FX, settlement / payout, engine execution, broad redesign, Settings / Copilot expansion, Evidence Register acceptance, Class B implementation, a second Lane V ticket, or scope outside the surfaces above. Rollback is a single revert or removal/disablement of the governed synthetic fixture path, with documentation evidence preserved for reconstruction. Lane N / Lane M / Lane R issues cannot override these controls.

### Archived Lane N brief — `NARRATIVE-006`

**Completed ticket:** `NARRATIVE-006` — Institutional Investor Orientation Document, Research R2 (Founder activation **§170**; Founder Research R1 disposition and bounded amendment **§171**; Founder Research R2 acceptance and closeout **§172**; Internal **D-055** / **D-056** / **§6f.19** completed).

**Objective:** Translate the accepted `NARRATIVE-005` Investor Orientation Brief into one calm, institutional, editorial-quality internal orientation document that helps a sophisticated evaluator understand Hedgr without turning the artifact into an investor deck, marketing collateral, or an investment solicitation.

**Primary uncertainty:** Can the accepted two-page source be expressed as an eight-page-maximum visual document while preserving its meaning, current implementation boundary, evidence discipline, and distinction between present proof and future ambition?

**Type / execution class:** Class A informational / non-authorising design expression. Green-eligible only after parallel-lane authority is verified in **D-055** / **§7** / **§7a**; Green classification does **not** independently activate or widen this ticket (ADR **0025** / **§6g**).

**Authority basis:** Founder acceptance of `NARRATIVE-005` and approval to execute this separately named design-expression sequence on 2026-07-27 + Internal **D-055** / **§6f.19** + activation **§170** + Founder approval on 2026-07-28 to accept Research R1 with bounded revisions and proceed with Research R2 + Internal **D-056** / **§171** + Founder review and approval of Research R2 on 2026-07-28 + closeout **§172** + accepted source `docs/strategy/investor/HEDGR_INVESTOR_ORIENTATION_BRIEF.md` + completed revision brief `docs/strategy/investor/HEDGR_INSTITUTIONAL_INVESTOR_ORIENTATION_DOCUMENT_R2_REVISION_BRIEF.md` + frozen Canonical Story v1.0, Customer One-Pager v1.0, and Narrative Style Guide v1.0 + governed brand authority.

**DRI:** Founder — @mhibajene.

**Artifact status:** Research R1 delivered, reviewed, and preserved; internal Research R2 design accepted; `NARRATIVE-006` completed; **no publication or circulation authority**.

**Maximum length:** Eight pages total.

**Required page architecture:**

1. Cover.
2. The Financial Stability Problem.
3. A New Category.
4. What Hedgr Is.
5. Institutional Philosophy.
6. What Exists Today.
7. Evidence.
8. Long-Term Vision.

**In scope / must:**

- Preserve the accepted `NARRATIVE-005` source meaning and current repo implementation truth.
- Preserve all approved Research R1 narrative copy. The bounded revision is visual and editorial except for the exact Founder-locked reader-facing metadata changes below.
- Use only approved Hedgr brand assets, typography, palette, and logo-derived geometric language.
- Use Paper as the reviewable design source and export one PDF review proof.
- Keep the narrative primary; use diagrams only to clarify relationships already present in the source.
- Distinguish clearly among current implementation, bounded evidence, institutional intent, and future vision.
- State the non-executing, non-custodial, no-customer-money boundary clearly.
- Replace the reader-facing footer on interior pages with exactly `Hedgr Investor Orientation Brief`.
- Change the page 8 running title to exactly `LONG-TERM VISION`.
- Remove the page 8 `THE EVALUATION QUESTION` label, the evaluation question, and the full-width navy evaluation panel.
- Recompose the reclaimed page 8 area as a controlled-ambition close led by the governed-core architecture and the existing final narrative statement.
- Keep all `INTERNAL RESEARCH ARTEFACT / PUBLICATION NOT AUTHORISED` status language.
- Record the Paper file locator and PDF proof status in `docs/strategy/investor/HEDGR_INSTITUTIONAL_INVESTOR_ORIENTATION_DOCUMENT_R2_REVIEW.md`.
- Reconcile `docs/ops/HEDGR_STATUS.md` / `docs/ops/NOTION_GOVERNANCE_STAGING.md` on activation and closeout only.

**Out of scope / must not:**

- Rewrite or expand the accepted brief or frozen narrative sources.
- Create or call the artifact an investor deck, pitch deck, fundraising deck, white paper, or marketing campaign.
- Claim fundraising traction, investor endorsement, market validation, product readiness, or institutional adoption.
- Imply live custody, deposits, withdrawals, conversion, settlement, automated allocation, yield routing, customer-money capability, or regulated advice.
- Include valuations, terms, projections, returns, performance claims, market sizing, or financial forecasts.
- Imply provider, jurisdiction, legal, regulatory, network, asset, or custody approval.
- Authorise external publication, distribution, website use, or broader fundraising materials.
- Alter Lane V scope, evidence, thresholds, decision rule, timing, stop conditions, rollback, or closeout.
- Generate, redraw, recolour, stretch, or substitute governed brand assets.

**Success signals:** A sophisticated reader can accurately explain the problem, Hedgr’s category and system centre, institutional posture, what exists today, the evidence supporting continued evaluation, what remains deliberately unbuilt, and the long-term vision—without interpreting the document as an investment offer or live-product claim.

**Failure signals:** The artifact reads like a pitch deck, marketing campaign, crypto product, yield proposition, or already-operational financial platform; visual expression changes source meaning; current and future capability blur; or any unapproved claim, asset treatment, or publication path appears.

**Stop condition:** Stop and return the artifact for Founder disposition if the design requires new narrative, unsupported evidence, a new contact channel, a product or trust-boundary interpretation, an unofficial asset, publication, or any scope beyond the accepted source and governed brand system.

**Closeout result:**

1. Paper source delivered against the Research R2 eight-page architecture and governed brand rules — **Pass**.
2. PDF review proof exported and visually checked — **Pass**.
3. Review record completed with source locators, brand QA, narrative-boundary QA, and unresolved inputs — **Pass**.
4. Founder disposition recorded — **Accept**.
5. Lane N closed; D-055 completed as active concurrency; singular-ticket restored for Lane V — **Pass under §172**.

**Rollback:** Revert the **§171** amendment and remove Research R2 artifacts; preserve the delivered Research R1 proof, `CLASS-A-VAL-002`, accepted `NARRATIVE-005`, and frozen narrative parents unchanged. If the underlying ticket is also revoked or closed, remove `NARRATIVE-006` from **§7** / **§7a**, complete or revoke D-055 as factually appropriate, restore singular-ticket posture, and reconcile staging.

**Authority basis:** Founder-approved Controlled Parallelism v2 (**§6f.2** / D-029) + this **§7** / **§7a** naming; builds on completed `FE-TRUST-001` (**§123**) and shipped read-only dashboard / withdraw trust baselines.

**Type:** Class A frontend / trust-surface presentation + synthetic fixtures (prototype-preferred).

**In scope:**
- A bounded, visibly synthetic withdrawal journey (prefer `apps/frontend/app/prototype/**` or an equivalently labelled non-default demo mount) composing `FE-TRUST-001` synthetic exception states with calm institutional withdrawal trust presentation.
- Explicit synthetic / non-live / non-production labelling so closed-user sessions cannot mistake the journey for live availability.
- Optional closed-user test facilitator notes under `docs/ops/` limited to claim boundaries and session labelling (no product-availability claims).
- Frontend Vitest coverage for synthetic labelling, disabled/non-executing primary actions where applicable, and trust-framing denylist continuity.
- Governed design tokens and existing design-system authority only.
- Status / Notion staging closeout updates only.

**Required UX posture:** Status before action; risk visibility; clear exit/support paths; no urgency; no guaranteed outcomes; no implied regulatory approval, provider availability, or settlement truth; no simulated state presented as live state; calm institutional presentation; tangibility without readiness (ADR **0023** / governance philosophy).

**Must not:**
- Enabled live withdrawal or deposit actions; provider branding; live provider availability.
- Live fees, live FX, live limits, real payout timing.
- “Instant” or “guaranteed” payout claims.
- Endpoint / regulatory / chain / stablecoin / market selection.
- Real-money receipts; executable transaction controls; production route activation of unavailable flows.
- Misleading success states; customer-money activity.
- Backend live coupling; Class B execution; Copilot directive/runtime binding.
- Widening Stability Engine posture semantics beyond existing read-only / informational ADRs.
- Treating user feedback as Class B authorization, market selection, or §7 sequencing authority.
- Connecting outputs to counsel send, provider outreach, or convergence-gate satisfaction.

**Dependencies:** None on Class B market/provider decisions or `PH-COMP-B-003` completion. Must not require Lane A outputs.

**Assumptions:** Synthetic states remain informational; closed-user feedback is exploration evidence only; work remains useful if no Class B rail is ever approved.

**Risks:** Mock UX drift toward implied product commitments; pressure to activate unavailable flows; over-broad ticket interpretation; legal-process timing incorrectly influencing UX research (mitigated by counsel DEFER).

**Stop conditions:** Surface implies live execution/availability; requires provider-specific behaviour; cannot keep synthetic labelling; validation/trust review fails; work begins driving market/provider selection; feedback treated as Class B authority.

**Expected behaviour:** Target users can inspect a labelled synthetic withdrawal journey and read-only dashboard context without any customer-money action becoming possible.

**Affected files / modules (expected):** `apps/frontend/app/prototype/**` and/or clearly labelled demo mounts; reuse of `apps/frontend/lib/tx/synthetic-exception-state.ts` / `SyntheticTxExceptionNotice`; corresponding `__tests__`; optional `docs/ops/**` facilitator/claim-boundary notes; status closeout only. Prefer avoiding shipped `(app)/withdraw` money-mutation polish unless required and still visibly non-live. Avoid `apps/frontend/lib/engine/**` unless a future separate ticket names it.

**Test / verification path:** Frontend Vitest for presentation contracts and synthetic labelling; no live network; preserve hermetic CI.

**Feature-flag / deny-default posture:** Synthetic/demo mounts must remain non-default or explicitly labelled; unknown execution state denies action; no production enablement of live money controls.

**Rollback path:** Revert the frontend PR; remove synthetic journey mount; no customer remediation; no provider offboarding.

**Acceptance criteria:**
- Visibly synthetic withdrawal journey merged with tests and explicit non-live labelling.
- No live deposit/withdraw enablement; no provider/rail/chain/stablecoin/endpoint/market selected.
- Remains truthful if no Class B rail is approved, provider/endpoint/market changes, Class B execution remains blocked, or prototype never connects to real money.
- Lane C closeout restores Lane C to idle without implying product availability or convergence-gate satisfaction.
- User-session feedback, if captured, is recorded as exploration evidence only.

**Non-authorization statement:** Completion of `FE-TRUST-002` does **not** satisfy the **§6e** convergence gate. UI completeness is not product availability. Closed-user testing is not Class B readiness. This ticket does not authorize Class B execution or customer-money activity.

### Archived brief — Lane B — Execution-neutral backend foundation — `BE-FOUND-001`

**Completed execution ticket:** **`BE-FOUND-001`** - Execution-neutral backend domain-error taxonomy and request-correlation primitives.

**Objective:** Implement the smallest coherent backend foundation slice that improves readiness without creating a financial execution path: a shared domain error taxonomy and request-correlation primitives on the existing Flask stub boundary, preserving deny-by-default and `STUB_MODE` posture.

**Execution class:** Class A / execution-neutral backend foundation (provider-independent; non-executing).

**Authority basis:** Founder-approved governed parallelism (**§6e** / D-026) + this **§7** / **§7a** naming.

**Scope (permitted):**
- Shared domain error taxonomy in `apps/backend/src/lib/` (extend or complement existing `errors.py` without provider-specific codes).
- Request correlation primitives (e.g. correlation-id / request-id helpers and structured error envelope fields) that do not execute transactions.
- Deterministic unit tests under `apps/backend/tests/` for taxonomy and correlation behaviour.
- Hermetic / stub-preserving documentation notes in `apps/backend/README.md` limited to the new primitives.
- Optional structured logging hooks that emit correlation ids without calling external providers.

**Architectural posture:** Stable core, volatile edges. Core may represent error states, authorization-denial shape, and correlation metadata. Volatile edge must contain all unresolved external assumptions (provider payloads, webhooks, rails, chain, stablecoin, FX, quotes, payout refs, settlement accounts, provider KYC/KYB, provider limits, provider refunds).

**Exclusions / must not:**
- Live provider SDKs; provider-specific adapters; live webhooks.
- Custody integration; wallet transaction execution; chain transactions; stablecoin conversion.
- Treasury workers; payout orchestration; real-money reconciliation.
- Production financial-provider credentials; automated compliance decisions.
- Executable deposits or withdrawals; customer-money movement; production settlement logic; accounting claims.
- Provider / market / rail / chain / stablecoin / endpoint selection by code or documentation implication.
- Widening `STUB_MODE=false` live paths; removing deny-by-default `501` guards on deposit/withdrawal routes.
- Connecting frontend live flows to new backend primitives as production truth.
- Class B execution; Class C automation.

**Dependencies:** None on Class B market/provider decisions. May proceed while `PH-COMP-B-002` remains active. Must not depend on `FE-TRUST-001` completion.

**Assumptions:** Backend abstractions can remain provider-independent; future adapters may require redesign after Class B findings; synthetic/stub behaviour remains non-authoritative for customer money.

**Risks:** Hidden provider assumptions in error codes; premature API contract lock-in; ticket-boundary overread.

**Edge cases / failure modes:** Unknown policy or execution state must deny by default; missing correlation id must not invent provider truth; taxonomy must not encode custody/settlement ownership.

**Expected behaviour:** Stub backend remains hermetic; new primitives are testable offline; deposit/withdrawal live paths remain unimplemented / denied outside stub records that do not move customer money.

**Affected files / modules (expected):** `apps/backend/src/lib/errors.py` (and/or new sibling modules under `apps/backend/src/lib/`); `apps/backend/tests/**`; optionally `apps/backend/README.md`; status closeout only in `docs/ops/HEDGR_STATUS.md` / `docs/ops/NOTION_GOVERNANCE_STAGING.md`.

**Test / verification path:** `cd apps/backend && source .venv/bin/activate && pytest` for new unit tests; no live network; CI hermetic posture preserved.

**Feature-flag / deny-default posture:** Preserve `STUB_MODE=true` default; no new live-mode enablement; unknown/unsupported execution remains denied.

**Rollback path:** Revert the backend PR; remove taxonomy/correlation modules; no customer remediation; no provider offboarding.

**Stop conditions:** Work requires provider-specific behaviour; schema encodes custody/conversion/settlement ownership; cannot remain deny-by-default; validation/security/trust review fails; ticket interpreted as authorizing live deposits/withdrawals.

**Acceptance criteria:**
- Domain error taxonomy and request-correlation primitives merged with tests.
- No provider/rail/chain/stablecoin/endpoint selected.
- No customer-money path introduced; stub deposit/withdrawal guards preserved.
- Useful even if Kenya remains thesis stress test, Philippines remains readiness benchmark, provider/endpoint/rail/chain/stablecoin changes, or Class B path pauses.
- Lane B closeout restores Lane B to idle without implying convergence-gate satisfaction.

**Non-authorization statement:** Completion of `BE-FOUND-001` does **not** satisfy the **§6e** convergence gate. Architecture readiness is not execution readiness. This ticket does not authorize Class B execution or customer-money activity.
